āryamañjuśrī nāmasaṅgītiḥ adhyeṣaṇā oṃ namo mañjuśrī kumārabhūtāya
atha vajradharaḥ śrīmān durdāntadamakaḥ paraḥ | triloka vijayī vīro guhyarāṭ kuliśeśvaraḥ || 1 ||
oṃ namo mañjunāthāya viṣayaviṣayivyomāśleṣapravṛtta nimittakaḥ raviśaśitamo vartmāvṛtyā śarādi 'calakriyam
sphuraduru' tarajñānajyoti śrutiśabarādhipaṃ maṇimayaśilārūḍhaṃ gūḍhaṃ namāmi nirāpa dam amṛta kaṇikāyamānaṃ sadgurupādaprasādato'dhigatam
' taddīpyate mamāsīta svasmṛtaye nāmasaṅgītau ||
iha khalu śrīdhānyakaṭake mahācaityasthāne' nānātantraśravaṇārthibhiradhyeṣitaḥ śrīśākya- siṃho nāma buddho bhagavān caitra pūrṇimāyāṃ śrīdharmadhātuvāgīśvara maṇḍalaṃ tadupari śrīmā nna- kṣatramaṇḍalamādibuddhaṃ visphārya tatra tasminneva dine buddhābhiṣekaṃ dattvā devādibhyaḥ sarvamantranīti bṛhallaghutantrabhedena deśitavān
uktañca śrībṛhadādibuddhe- · gṛdhrakūṭe yathā śāstrā prajñāpāramitā naye | tathā mantranaṃye proktā śrīdhānye dharmadeśanā || iti
tatra ceyameva nāmasaṅgītiḥ paramaduravagāhaparamārthaniryāsādhikaraṇatvena sarva- mantranaya pradhānabhūtā

āryamañjuśrīnāmasaṅgīti: hṛdayabhūtaṃ tathāgatena 'saṃprakāśitam, ṣaṭ kulanāḍīsamanvitaṣaṭcakravyavasthitadvāṣaṣṭyadhika- śatanāḍī nirodhā viśuddhayā dvāṣaṣṭyadhikaśataślokaḥ, śeṣaiścānuśaṃsādikamiti
tatra tāvat, atha vastradharaḥ śrīmānityādiṣoḍaśaślokairadhyeṣaṇāvyājena tadeva paramākṣaramāha- atheti
akāreṇātra nairātmyapratipādakatvena sarvākāravaropetā śūnyatā proktā
thakāreṇāpyakṣobhya svabhāvapratipādanena nirālambakaruṇā
etacca suviśadaṃ saṃpuṭa ( suviśadasphuṭaṃ ) hevajraṭīkāyāṃ vyākhyātam
tayoradvedhāt maṇivaraṭakāntaḥ sthita- sahajānanda śukrameva śabdābhidheyam, athetyucyate
uktañca - madhyavaṃkāraḥ sarva buddhasukhālayaḥ | khadhātau vajrasattvo'yaṃ kāyavākcittayogataḥ || kāyo bindvinduḥ śukraṃ ca vāg visargo rajo raviḥ | cittākārāstvamī proktā evaṃdhāto vyavasthitāḥ || iti
ata eva śūnyatākaruṇābhinnaṃ mahāsukhajñānavastraṃ tādātmyena dharatīti vajradharaḥ
vajramabhedyajñānamasatsaṃkalpāsthita skandhakleśamṛtyuvighnamārerabhedyatvāt
evoktam- māraḥ svacittaṃ na paro'sti mārastathāgataṃ na tathāgatānām | mārasya bhaṃgo vijayo muniśca cittena cittapratibodha eva ||
ata tat sūcakaṃ pañca śū cikavastraṃ bahiḥ tadīyastattva sūcanārthaṃ dharatīti vā vajradharaḥ
matvartha: śrīradvayaṃ jñānaṃ tadanubhavarūpatvena tādātmyena nityayogāt śrīmān śrutamiti mayārthaḥ
taduktam- evaṃ “vastrābjamadhyāntavastrī tiṣṭhati bindughṛk | sa evā vyatibhinnātmā mayeti prakaṭīkṛtam ||

śrīādibuddha
amṛta kaṇikāsahitā tantre'pyevaṃ mayā yat śrutamiti vacanaṃ tanmayā jñātameva vastrī candradravādyaḥ śirasi gala' hṛdabje ca nābhau ca guhye
vajrastrīṇāṃ bhage tatparakamalagate 'bindumokṣatrayeṇa buddhakṣetre' praviṣṭaḥ tadiha sa bhagavān yogibhirveditavyaḥ || iti
atra ca vajradharoṃ nānyo'pi tu mahāvajradhara eva
caturaśītidharma skandhasahasrāṇā- manya[tama]sya sakṛdudgrahaṇārthasāmarthyāttaduktam - vyākhyātāhamahaṃ dharmaḥ śrotāhaṃ svagaṇairyutaḥ | sādhyo'haṃ jagataḥ śāstā' loko'haṃ laukiko'pyaham || sahajānandasvabhāvo'haṃ paramāntaṃ viramādikam | bhāvo'haṃ naiva bhāvo'haṃ buddho'haṃ vastubodhanāt || māṃ na jānanti ye mūḍhāḥ kosīdyopahatāśca ye | vihare'haṃ sukhāvatyāṃ sadvajjrayoṣito bhage || ādikamakasattvāvataraṇāya tu buddhanāṭakamadhyeṣakasaṅgītikārādika miti
hariharahiraṇyagarbhādibhirdamayitumaśakyatvāt durdāntāḥ, ṣaṭśatādhike kaviṃśatisahasraśvāsa- praśvāsaḥ, teṣāṃ paramākṣarajñāna mahārasābiddhatayā tadrūpopaghātād durdāntadamakaḥ, para yogī prāṇātipātaṃ dinaniśikurute prāṇanāśaḥ sa uktaḥ
sarvajñapadalābhāya na bāhye prāṇātipātaḥ
bāhye yaḥ prāṇātipāta ukto durdāntadamanāya sa teṣāṃ yogabalenākṛṣṭaḥ
punastasminneva kāye praveśanīyo yogineti kātri
rasabiddhatayā
bāhyādayaḥ
tadukta āryamañjuśrī nāmasaṅgītiḥ durdāntadamako bhavati, na tu durdāntāntaka iti
trayo lokāstrilokam, kāyavāk- cittam, tadvijetuṃ vaśayituṃ jñānena sahaikalolīkatu ṃ śīlaṃ yasya sa tathā
uktañca- caturbuddhāsanāsīnaṃ vajrapadmasusaṃsthitam | anubhūtaṃ krameṇaiva caturānandalakṣaṇam || evaṃ vyāpyavyāpakasambandhena kāyavākcittajñānabhedena caturvidhaṃ paramākṣarasukham
uktañca- bhage liṅga pratiṣṭhāpya bodhicittaṃ na cotsṛjet | bhāvayed buddhabimbaṃ tu traidhātukamaśeṣataḥ || iti
vigata 'ravaścaladrūpaśvāsavāto yasya sa tathā
guhyaṃ śrāvaka - pratyekabuddhayānayo- ruttaraṃ vajrayānaṃ kāyavākcittajñānekalolībhāvo vā tatra mahāsukharūpatayā rājata iti guhyarāṭ
pañcaguhyarājatvād vā guhyarāṭ
uktañcādibuddhena - yogī prāṇātipātaṃ dinaniśi kurute prāṇanāśaḥ sa uktaḥ yaḥ śabdo vaktrahīnaḥ prabhavati hṛdaye'sau mṛṣāvāda eva | sarvajñajñānabhūmergrahaṇamapi ca yad yoginaḥ steyamuktaṃ saukhyaṃ bindu (dva) prapāte bhavati ca paradārasya sevā'virāgāt || prāṇāyāmānalena dravamapi śaśinaḥ pānakaṃ madyapānam uṣṇīṣe'ṅguṣṭhaparvād vrajati tithivaśāt pūrṇimāntaṃ svacittam
uṣṇīṣādaṅgulīṣu vrajati punaridaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣāvasāne
sā caryā yogino vai pratidinasamaye tviṣṭasiddhipradā yā ||
vi (dvi) kalparāje - - prāṇinaśca tvayā ghātyā vaktavyañca mṛṣāvacaḥ |
adattañca tvayā grāhyaṃ sevanaṃ parayoṣitaḥ ||
ekacittaṃ prāṇivadhaṃ prāṇaścittaṃ yato matam |
sattvānuttārayiṣyāmi mṛṣāvādaṃ ca śabditam ||
yoṣicchukrama dattañca paradārāḥ svābhasundarīti ||

maddhitāya vajramaṇivaraṭakānubhūtasukhānubandhāya
ata eva mamārthāya sarvasattvānāṃ tatsukhotpādāya
tathatārūpeṇābhinnatvena tatsukhotpādanameva matprayojanam
anukampāya svapareṣāmasatsaṃkalpaprakṣālanāya
mahāsukhasvabhāvena trailokyavyāpakatvena vibho iti sambodhanam
ṣoḍaśakāyānandādibindunirodhena prādeśikaskandhadhātvāyata- nānāṃ nirodhād māyājālavanniḥsaṃgaṃ sukhatvena trailokyasyābhisambodhiḥ sākṣātkā- rastasyāḥ lābhī san tādātmyena
ata evāha - akāra - haṃkārasaṃpuṭatvena yuganaddharūpo bhavāmi yathā, tathā nāmasaṅgīti prakāśayatu sambuddha iti sambandhaḥ ||

ajñānapaṅkamagnānāṃ hitāya ajñāneti
ajñānamavidyāvāsanā tadeva paṅkaḥ dustaratvena tanmagnānāṃ hitāya mahāsukhollāsāya
kleśa cyutiduḥkham tena vyākulacetasāṃ ṣaḍgatibhramaṇaśīlānā- manuttaramavācyaṃ prakṛtiprabhāsvaraṃ mahāsukhaṃ phalaṃ tatprāptaye prakāśayatviti vakṣyamāṇena sambandhaḥ || 8 ||
prakāśayatu saṃbuddho bhagavān śāstā jagadguruḥ
mahāsamayatattvajña indriyāśayavitparaḥ || 9||
prakāśayatviti
sahajasukhollāsena sarvadharmāvabodhāt sambuddhaḥ
bhagaḥ sarvākāra- nirākāraśūnyatā, tadyogād bhagavān
ṣaṭśatādhikaikaviṃśatisahasraśvāsānāṃ mahāsukhā- ārya mañjuśrīnāmasaṅgītiḥ bhinnatvena śāsanāt śāstā
ata eva jagatāṃ śvāsavātānāṃ kāyavākcittānāṃ vā gurustattvopadeṣṭā
mahāsamayacaturthabindustasya tadeva vā tasvaṃ 'sukhajñānaṃ tattādātmyena jānātoti tathā
indriyāṇāṃ cakṣurādīnāṃ grāhyagrāhaka bhāvāpannānāmāśayaṃ sukhaṃ prakṛtimabhedena vettītīndriyāśayavit, ataeva paro lokātikrāntaḥ ||
bhagavan jñānakāyasya mahoṣṇīṣasya goṣpateḥ
mañjuśrījñānasattvasya jñānamūrteḥ svayambhuvaḥ || 10 ||
bhagavanniti
bhagavanniti sambodhanaṃ jñānakāyaścaturthakāyaḥ ṣoḍaśārddhārddha bindudhṛk
tasyāpatitabodhicittabindutvena vivṛtyordhvagamanenoṣṇīṣasthānagatatvāt mahoṣṇīṣasya, gordhvani: ṣaḍakṣarātmaka ekāra ādhārātmakaḥ tasya patiḥ ṣaṣṭho vaṃkāro vajradharaḥ ādheya- svabhāvaḥ
tathā ca vakṣyati - pañcākṣaro mahāśūnyo binduśūnyaḥ ṣaḍakṣaraḥ || iti
ekāra - vaṃkārarūpa ityarthaḥ
mañjuśrījñānasattvasyeti dharmatāsaṃketena mañjuśabdena vajraśikhare protphulla'varakamalaṃ tadevādvayajñānāśrayatvāt śrīstadutpannamadvayajñānaṃ mañju- śrījñānasattvasya
sarvasattvānāṃ hṛdayavihāritvāt 'jñānamūrteḥ
svayamātmanā hetunira- pekṣatvād bhavati, sākṣād bhavatīti svayambhuvaḥ
tadvijñānaṃ na kenacitkriyate na kenāpi dīyate sarvākārāntargataṃ sarvarūpamakṛtrimatayā svasaṃvedyam
taduktam — na sahajaṃ kenaciddattaṃ na kasmāccāgataṃ yataḥ
ātmanā jñāyate puṇyād guru parvopasevayā ||
gambhīrārthāmudārārthī mahārthāmasamāṃ śivām
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇīṃ nāmasaṅgītimuttamām || 11 ||
gambhīreti

mahāsukhādhāratayā vajramadvayajñānameva khaṅgaḥ sarva- kleśaniṣūdanatvādyasya sa tathā
ata eva paramavāsanāṃ nikṛntatīti nikṛntanaḥ
pañca- varṇaprāṇādivāyuvaśvavajram
taduktam- pañcajñānamayaṃ śvāsaṃ pañcabhūtasvabhāvakam
niścārya yannāsāgre piṇḍarūpeṇa kalpayet
pañcavarṇamahāratnaṃ prāṇāyāma iti sthi (smṛtam

pañcavarṇamayaṃ śvāsaṃ prāṇāyāmasya kalpanā
nāsikāgre prayatnena bhāvayedyogavit sadā

6
vajrajvālākarālākṣo vajrajvālāśiroruhaḥ
vajrāveśo mahāveśaḥ śatākṣo vajralocanaḥ
7
vajrajvāleti
'jñānacaṇḍālījvalanādvajrajvālayā ādarśapratisenātulyayā karālaṃ danturaṃ akṣimāṃsādicakṣurmahāsukhadarśanaṃ yasya sa tathā
vajrajvālāvadabhedyatayā śirasi rohatīti vajrajvālāśiroruhaḥ
mahāsukhatayā uṣṇīṣakramaṇād vajrasya jñāna - āryamañjuśrīnāmasaṅgītiḥ vajrasya prāṇāpānanirodhena dhvananakampanādinādhiṣṭhānaṃ tadyogād vajrāveśaḥ traidhātukamahā- mudrābhiṣekadāyītyarthaḥ
mahāsukhatayā sarvabhāvasvabhāvatvād mahāveśaḥ
sarvadṛṣṭirahitatathā divyasparśasukhāvattyānu 'bhavajñānatvāt śatākṣaḥ
māṃsādicakṣurādeḥ viśvadarśanāvyāhata- darśanād vajralocanaḥ
7
vajraromāṅkuratanurvajña romaika vigrahaḥ vajra koṭinakhārambho vajrasāraghanacchaviḥ
8
vajrometi
sārddhatrikoṭi romābhyantaraśukravahanāḍikānāmaṅkaraḥ prakṛtyābhāsa: sa eva tanurābhogeṇa (tanuranābhogena) jñānādyasya vajromā suratanuḥ
vajramabhedyajñānaṃ tadeva romeva roma nirantarodgamanād ekamadvitīyaṃ śarīraṃ vigraho yasya sa tathā
sahajākāśatanurityarthaḥ
vajrakoṭī nakhā iva raktaśvetaguṇayogād nakhāḥ sahajānanda- bindavaḥ, terārabhyata iti vajñakoṭinakhārambhaḥ, jñānakāya ityarthaḥ
vajrasārā nirvikārā ghanā nirantarā sukhatvena lambamānā chaviḥ sparśasukhātmikā vaṣtrajihvānāḍī yasya sa tathā
8
vajramālāgharaḥ śrīmān vajrābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ
hāhāṭṭahāso nirghoṣo vajraghoṣaḥ ṣaḍakṣaraḥ
9
vajramālādhareti
vastraṃ pīṭhopapīṭhādiśarīrasthānaṃ layabhogādikrameṇa sahajo- tpattirvajramālāsahajabindumālā tāṃ dharatīti vajramālādharaḥ
śrīmān [a] dvaitajñānī
sarvāṅgavyāpiparamākṣarasukhaśukrarūpatayā vajrābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ
divyāṅganāliṅgana- saṃbhūtasukhadhvanirhāhā asya aṭṭahāso vikāso yasya sa tathā
nirgato bahirghoṣānnirghoṣaḥ, antaḥ sukhasvayambhūḥ sukhanāda ityarthaḥ
uktañca - viyadgaṃgātīre taraṇiśaśabhṛnmadhyavasato gurorājñāleśādubhayapavana: svatanuvidhī
yayoryoge cittādyanubhavasukhasvādara sikāḥ yamantarvīkṣante kamapi sukhanādaḥ sa jayati
sūryendudyutikarbarīkṛta mahāsandhyānubandhidhiyo yasyānuttarakālalālitajagattādātmyamāpadyate yaścābhāsani (ti) kāminī samarasakrīḍāsukhai kadhvaniḥ jñānātmā sa tanurjayī vijayate sambhogakāyo jinaḥ
amṛta kaṇikāsahitā tāni vyañjanakāni tāśca rucayaste ṣoḍaśāpi svarāḥ visphūrjjanti ca saṃharanti ca yata: saṃlīyamānā api
āpātālatalocchalatkalakala: sambhogabhūmīśvaro brahmastambhavijṛmbhito vijayate vajrī nabho dundubhiḥ || iti
kāyo'pi sarvākāśavyāpi mahāsukhanā datvādvajñaghoṣaḥ
ṣaṭcakreṣu akṣarasukha- vedanāt ṣaḍakṣaraḥ || 9
mañjughoṣo mahānādastrailokyaikaravo mahān
ākāśadhātuparyantaghoṣo ghoṣavatāṃ varaḥ ||10
iti ādarśajñānagāthā: ' pādena sādhaṃ daśa
mañjughoṣeti
sarvacakreṣvāgata binduprāṇasa nmiśrajñānavijñāne kalolī bhūtanādatvād mañjughoṣo mañjuśrīḥ
vajrakamalakaṇikākāśagata 'stathānādarūpatvānmahānādaḥ
trailokyamālokādyābhāsatrayaṃ śūnyātiśūnyekekīkaraṇāt
āsphānakasamādhinā ekamadvitīyaṃ tadeva ravaḥ sarvaśūnyaprabhāsvaraṃ yasya sa tathā
ata eva mahān
cakṣurādīnāṃ niḥsvabhāvatayā sarvadharmāṇāṃ ākāśadhātuparyantaṃ niṣṭhā yasya sa ghoṣaḥ sukhanādo yasya sa tathā
ni:svabhāvatā sarvadharmāṇāṃ ghoṣavatāṃ varaḥ, maṇivaraṭakoṣṇīṣavartī bhagavān sukhabinduriti || 10
hṛdgataprāṇavāhināḍī saptakaviṇmūtraśukraśoṇitavāhinā ḍicatuṣṭaka- nirāvaraṇatāpratipādakasapādadaśaślokyā ādarśajñānasya lokottarasatyarūpaskandhavairocanamukhena vyākhyā ||7
pratyavekṣaṇājñānam tathatābhūtane rātmya bhūta koṭiranakṣaraḥ śūnyatā' vādivṛṣabho gambhīrodāragarjanaḥ ||1
tathatetyādinā jñānārci: suprabhāsvara iti paryantena dvayādhikacatvāriṃśacchlokyā suviśuddhasaṃjñāskandhāmitābhadvāreṇa pratyavekṣaṇājñānastutimāha - tathateti
tathatā vāma- dakṣiṇavāhabhaṅgenāvadhūtīvāhaḥ
uktañca– nāḍikādisvabhāvena devatātattvayogataḥ
tāsāmetatparaṃ śuddhaṃ svarūpaṃ niḥsvabhāvatā ||
yatprajñopāyayoraikyaṃ sarvākāraikasaṃvaram
sāvadhūtī vidhūtātmā madhyamā pratipanmatā ||
ādimadhyāntasaṃkalpasambandhānavadhānataḥ 'śuddhaḥ sphaṭikasaṃkāśaḥ prakāśaḥ so'vadhūtikaḥ ||
seva bhūtānāṃ pṛthivyādīnāṃ nairātmyaṃ ātmātmīyānupalambhaḥ
tadeva bhūtakoṭiryathā- bhūtaniṣṭhā yasya sa tathā tanniṣṭha sukha svabhāva ityarthaḥ
acyutakaruṇārasapūrṇatvādanakṣaraḥ
kāyavākcittanirodhena svasaṃvedyasukhaprāptiḥ śūnyatā, tatprakāśanāt śūnyatāvādī
sarvamārārividhvaṃsa dhurandharatvād vṛṣabhaḥ
ekapadaṃ vā
gambhīrodārasya śūnyatākaruṇā- bhinnasya 'sukhasya garjanamullāso yasya sa tathā
tathā ca vimalaprabhā śūnyasya bhāvaḥ śūnyatā atītānāgataṃ jñeyaṃ śūnyaṃ, tasya darśanaṃ bhāvaḥ śūnyatā
gambhīrodārā'tītā- nāgatābhāvād gambhīrā
atītānāgatadarśanādudāreti || 1
dharmaśaṅkho mahāśabdo dhamaṃgaṇḍī mahāraṇaḥ
apratiṣṭhitanirvāṇo daśadigdharmadundubhiḥ ||2
śuddha
amṛtakaṇikāsahitā dharmaśaṅkhati
dharmodayod 'bhūtatvāt pratiśrutkopamadharmadeśanācca dharmaḥ, śaṅkhavannirma- latvācca śaṅkhaḥ
mahāsukhabindumayo manorājaḥ
padmāntarālena nāsikāyāṃ vajro- dbhavaṃ kṣīrasamudrarūpaṃ cintāmaṇi sarvajagatsvabhāvaṃ śrīherukaṃ vedmi mahāsamudramityukteḥ
buddhatvadāyakaniḥsvabhāvasukhanādarūpatvānmahāśabdaḥ
sakalasukhamayavāgvajrasamādhivisphā- ritasarvadharmaprakāśakadharmajyotiṃ rūpatvād gharmagaṇḍī
anupamasukhanāda dharmaraṇa- nānmahāraṇa: viramānandaḥ saṃsāratvāt pratiṣṭhitaḥ
'maṇyagrātpatanānnirvāṇaṃ tadabhāvānna vidyate pratiṣṭhitañca nirvāṇañca yasya sa tathā
anuttarānandadharma sukhakalyāṇaprakāśanād daśadigdhamaṃdundubhiḥ || 2
arūpo rūpavāna gyo nānārūpo manomayaḥ
sarvarūpāvabhāsaśrī raśeṣapratibimbadhṛk ... ||3
arūpeti
ākāśaniṣṭhatayā sarvacittacetasikāvidyā pratibhāsanirodhānna vidyate prakṛtisvarūpātiriktaṃ rūpaṃ yasya sa tathā
dharmaṃkāyarūpakāyai kalolībhāvādanāvila- rūpatvādrūpavān
hṛdayasukhakarasūryamaṇḍalā ghāratvena sarvamārapramathanaśīlatvādagyaḥ
dvāsaptatināḍīsahasreṣu prakṛtirūpeṇa sukhadharmadhāturūpanispandarūpatvānnānārūpaḥ
ekakṣaṇā- bhisaṃbodhirūpatvānmanomayaḥ
nirāsaṅgatayā pṛthivyaptejovāyvākāśacakṣuḥ śrotaghrāṇa- jilhvākāyamanasāṃ pratyātmavedyatayā pratibhāsanāt sarvarūpāva bhāsaśroḥ
viśvabimba- darśanena kṛṣṇarekhāyāmanantānantajñānakāyābhinnasambhogakāyapratibhāsanād aśeṣa- pratibimbadhṛk
uktañca- yaddhemarūpyamaṇiśaṅkhaśilāprabāla- vaiḍūryatāmrarajatādiṣu saṃsthitaśca
tatraka eva hi śaśī gaganodarānta- yavartamāna vapuṣā pratibhāti yadvat ||
evaṃ sa nāthaḥ khalu cittavajjo nānāvidhastiṣṭhati jantuvarge
sa cāvyayaḥ svātmani viśvarūpo māyāmayo vyāptasamastalokaḥ || iti ||3
āryamañjuśrīnāmasaṅgītiḥ apradhṛṣyo maheśākhyastredhātukamaheśvaraḥ
samucchritāyaṃ mārgastho dharmaketurmahodayaḥ || 4
mapradhṛṣyeti
sarvavikalpāsaṃhāryatvādapradhṛṣyaḥ
advayapuṇyajñānasambhāraniṣṭhā- rūpatvānmaheśākhyaḥ
traidhātukaṃ dharmasaṃbhoganirmāṇākhyaṃ tatra maheśvaro jñānakāyaḥ
samucchito'tyucco vikalparahitatvāt āryaḥ
mṛgyaṃte iti mārgaḥ ṣaṭcakra kamala- kaṇikā tatra mahāsukharūpatayā sthitatvāt samucchritāyaṃmāgaṃsthaḥ
maṇi varaṭake sakala- kleśavijayitvāddharmasya tattvaratnasya keturavācyasukhatvācca dhvajaḥ dharma ketuḥ
dharmasukhā- dvaitatayā ākāśadhātusthāmeyabuddha bimbānantapratibhāsatayā mahānudaya: 'sukhasya samullāso yasya sa tathā || 4
trailokyekakumārāṅgaḥ sthaviro vṛddhaḥ prajāpatiḥ
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇagharaḥ kāntastrailokya sundaraḥ ||5
trailokyaikakumārāṅgeti
trailokyaṃ kāyatrayam, tadekastadabhinno mahāsukhakāyaḥ, sa eva kumāro dharmadhāturūpeṇa vikārābhāvāt sa evāṅgaṃ sarvotpattiheturyasya sa trailokyaikakumārāṅgaḥ
divyamudrāsamudbhūtasukharūpa ityarthaḥ
pūrvoktamudrāprakarṣagatatvāt sarvasiddhijyeṣṭhatvācca sthavira ityucyate, mahārājasamādhikrameṇa
buddhajñānamayatvād vṛddhaḥ
jñānaprabodhena turyātītaprabhāsvaramayajñānabimbamayatvena sarvajantūnāmutpādanātprajānāṃ jantūnāṃ patiḥ prajāpatiḥ
śuklapratipadādipaścadaśakalātikrāntaṣoḍaśakalādaghekalakṣaṇa- rūpatvāt dvātriṃśa lakṣyanta iti lakṣaṇāni tāni dharatīti dvātriśallakṣaṇadharaḥ
saṃvṛti- vivṛtikrameṇa ṣoḍaśānandadvayadhāraṇādvā tathā
sarvadharmaikarasatvena sarvajanābhi laṣaṇīya tvāt kāntaḥ
svādhiṣṭhānasphuṭībhāvena secanakarūpatvāt trailokyena trailokyatvena sundaraḥ
uktañca— dvātriṃśallakṣaṇī śāstā maśītyanuvyañjanī prabhuḥ
yoṣidbhage sukhāvatyāṃ śukranāmnā vyavasthitaḥ ||
kṛtaṃ nairmāṇikaṃ kāyaṃ kāya tataśca mahatā tena sarvasattvānukampayā
dvātriṃśadvaralakṣaṇam ||
māna māgyaṃta padāni
lakṣaṃ tu, lāṣaṇīya punaścoktam- amṛtakaṇikā sahitā binā tena na saukhyaṃ syāt sukhaṃ hitvā bhavenna saḥ
śukrākāro bhaved bhagavān tatsukhaṃ kāminī smṛtam ||
na tat pratyekabuddhānāṃ śrāvakāṇāñca gocaram
gocaraṃ buddhaputrāṇāṃ vajrapadmodbhavaṃ padam || iti
pañcabuddhātmakaṃ sarvajago'yaṃ paśyatu ku nāṭakadivyaṃ
ehū so paramamahāsukhanāmā nṛtyati ekamanekarasena || iti
lokajñānaguṇācāryo lokācāryo viśāradaḥ
nāthastrātā trilokātaḥ śaraṇaṃ tāyī niruttaraḥ || *
lokānāṃ cakṣurādīnāṃ jñānaṃ nirāvaraṇapratibhāsastasya guṇaḥ pañca- kāmopabhogena saṃsāra doṣā'nupalepastasyācārya upadeṣṭā lokajñānaguṇācāryaḥ
yathā- bhavyatayā caturthābhiṣekaparyantadānāllokācāryaḥ
nānārūpaspharaṇena jaṃgadaryakaraṇā- virāmādviśārado nirbhayaḥ, abhedya sukhajñāna ityarthaḥ
'advayayogena susthitatvānnāyaḥ
trātā paramārthatrāṇīya saṃvṛtisatye karūpatayā sadā'cyutarūpatvāstrātā
sarvākāraśūnyatā- prakāśarūpatvena cyavanasukha grahagrastānāṃ nirmalajñānopadeśopasthito'pi trailokya āptaḥ, pañcakāmopabhoge'pi kṣīṇadoṣaḥ trilokāptaḥ
pañcabhūtānāṃ mahāsukha niṣṭhīkaraṇāt śaraṇaṃ
tāyī sukhamayajñānasantānaḥ tasyānupacchedena yogāttāyī, sanātanaśca- turthakṣaṇa rūpa ityarthaḥ
trailokyavyāpaka sahajānandarūpatvānniruttaraḥ ||
gaganābhoga sambhogaḥ sarvajñajñānasāgaraḥ
avidyāṇḍakośa saṃbhettā bhavapañjaradāraṇaḥ ||
gaganaṃ kamalakuliśasaṃyoge varaṭakā kāśadeśastasyābhogo vistārastatra samyagbhogo'dvayasukhabhuñjanaṃ yasya sa tathā sa gaganābhogasaṃbhogaḥ
kāyavākcitta- prahāṇātmaka mahāvajradharajñānaratnākaratvāt sarvajñajñānasāgaraḥ
avidyā saṃsāravāsanā sevāṇḍakoṣo jagadutpattihetutvāt
tasya sambhetā mahāsukhaikatāpādakaḥ
bhavaḥ kāyavāk- bhettā kāśastasyāḥ
avidyāṇḍa āryamañjuśrīnāmasaṅgītiḥ citta 'svarūpāparijñānaṃ sa eva pañjaro'nirgamahetutvāt
taddārayatīti bhavapañjara- dāraṇaḥ ||
śamitāśeṣa saṃkleśa: jñānābhiṣekamukuṭaḥ saṃsārārṇavapāragaḥ
samyak saṃbuddhabhūṣaṇaḥ ||
śamiteti
śamitā nirāvaraṇatāṃ nītā aśeṣāḥ savāsanāḥ saṃkleśā avidyā- didvādaśāṅgāni yena sa tathā pañcakāmavaśīkṛtaḥ
pañcopādānaskandhāḥ saṃsāraḥ, sa evārṇavaḥ rāgādivyākulatvāt, tasya pāraṃ nirāvaraṇaskandhādipratilambhastatra gacchatīti saṃsārārṇavapāragaḥ
jñānaṃ caturthānandarūpam, tadevābhiṣicyate nirāvaraṇī kriyate skandhadhātvādikamanenetyabhiṣekaścaturtho bindussa eva bindubhūṣaṇamasya sa tathā, samyak saṃbuddhān vā bhūṣayatīti tathā
anena caturthābhiṣekarūpo bhagavān uktaḥ
uktañca- karmamudrāprasaṅge'pi rakṣaṇīyaṃ mahāsaukhyaṃ jñānamudrānurāgiṇe
bodhicittaṃ dṛḍhavrataiḥ ||
anena rakṣiteneva buddhatvamiha janmani
amṛta kaṇikāsahitā evaṃ cittaṃ caturdhā 'trividhabhavagataṃ prāṇināṃ bindumadhye yogīndraḥ rakṣaṇīyaṃ sa masukhaphaladaṃ vyāpakaṃ mokṣahetoḥ
bindormokṣe kva mokṣo gataparama sukhe yogināṃ janmabīje tasmātsaṃsārasaukhyaṃ kṣaṇa iha yatibhiḥ sarvadā varjanīyaḥ || iti
samāje coktam - patite bodhicitte tu sarvasiddhi nidhāna ke
mūcchite skandhavijñāne kutaḥ siddhiraninditā ||
vimalaprabhāyāṃ ca - śrījñānavantrasamuccayayogatantre ca- śīlasambhārasampūrṇa puṇyajñānasamanvitam ||
daśa pāramitāḥ prāptāḥ 'sambuddhāstryadhvavartinaḥ anena sarvasaṃbuddherdharmacakraṃ pravartitam ||
vātaiḥ saṃghaṭṭamānaistaḍidanalaśikhā drāvayenmūni candraṃ yo yo bindurdvato'smādgalahṛdayagato nābhiguhye niruddhaḥ
bindoḥ spandadravaṃ yat kuliśamaṇigataṃ sanniruddhaṃ dhvajāgre prajñā 'jñānakṣaṇaṃ tadyadi dadati sukhaṃ bindu mālācyutena ||
jāgratsvapnasvarūpaṃ punaraparamidaṃ suptatūryasvabhāvaṃ kāya sthaṃ śvāsalīnaṃ vicarati viṣayān niścalaṃ cittalīnam
jñānasthaṃ strīprasaṅgāt kṣaṇamapi ca bhaved bodhicitte drute ca nirmāṇādeḥ krameṇa prabhavati niyataṃ cittavaścaturddhā ||

kṣarati prajñāsaṃge yasya sitaṃ tasya kena sukhavṛddhiḥ
kusumaṃ vasantasamaye patati phalaṃ kena cūtasya || iti
sarvāsāmeva māyānāṃ strīmāyaiva praśasyate
jñānatrayaprabhedo'yaṃ sphuṭamatraiva lakṣyate || iti

||24||
anādīti
dharmadhātujñānarūpatvena na vidyete ādinidhane utpādanirodhī viramakṣaṇacyutikṣaṇo yasya sa tathā
sarvapadārthānāṃ samatayā 'vagamād buddhaḥ
sāṃkle- śikavaiyavadānikadharmāṇāṃ paramārthato'bhāvenākāro niṣedhārthaḥ
tenādibuddhaḥ
ekakṣaṇa- pañcākāraviṃśatyākāramāyājālābhisambodhilakṣaṇaṃ sukhamādibuddha ityarthaḥ
nirasto- 'nvayaḥ prajñopāyātmako grāhyagrāhakalakṣaṇo dharmo yena sa tathā
jñānamadvaya jñānaṃ tadevekamadvitīyaṃ cakṣussarvadharma sukhardāśatvādyasya sa tathā
sakalavikalpaprahīṇatvā 'bamalaḥ
jñānaṃ śūnyatājñānaṃ tadeva bhūtiḥ sahajasukhāvabhāso yasya sa tathā
tathā tathatā karmādimudrā tatra gatamānandādisukhajñānaṃ yasya sa tathāgataḥ
|| 24 ||
vāgīśvaro mahāvādī vādirāḍ vādipuṅgavaḥ
vadatāṃ varo 'variṣṭho vādisiṃho'parājitaḥ
|| 25||
vāgīśvareti
anāhatadhvanisamullāsena sarvacittakṣaṇarūpabuddhānāmāpyāyanā- 'dvāgīśvaraḥ
manthamanthānayogena sarvadā ākāramullāsitvād vādināmmahanīyatvā- nmahāvādī
manthanīyasarvanāḍīṣu manyānarūpaprāṇavāyuprayogeṇa bodhicitta dhārayā sarvendriyadhātupuṣṭikārīti yāvat
divyamudrāprayogeṇa śrāvakādinirvāṇanirākaraṇā- dvādirāṭ
advayajñānavāditvād vādipuṅgavaḥ
samyak sarvadharmāvabodhādvavatāṃvaraḥ

|| 26 ||
samanteti
samantāddraṣṭuṃ śīlaṃ yasyāsī samantadarśī
samantabhadrarūpeṇa tad jñānalābhī, rāgavinayasattvāpekṣayā
advayadharmadhāturūpeṇa mahārāgastatra spharaṇāt
mahāsukhadhāraṇayā paramaprāmodyalābhāt prāmodyaḥ
tejomālā sahajacaṇḍālījyotiḥ- 'prabandhastādātmyena tadyogāttejobhālī
śobhanaṃ darśanaṃ sahajasukhasākṣātkāro yasya sa tathā
śūnyatā karuṇā'bhinnamahāsukhajñānāmṛtapūrṇatvād jñānasāgara ityarthaḥ
sakalajaga- dutpatti'kāraṇānanyasādhāraṇasarvajñatābīja prarohabhūtatathāgatalakṣaṇayogācchrovatsaḥ
śobhanā prabhā sahajajñānaspharaṇātmikā yasya sa tathā
anantalokadhātusthita buddhādyava- bhāsakaraṇāddīptiḥ
bhābhirbhāsukarā puñjarūpā yamāntakādikrodhāstatspharaṇarūpā dyutistaskrodhavinayasattvārthaṃ yasya sa tathā
|| 26 ||
mahābhiṣagvaraḥ śreṣṭhaḥ śalyaharttā niruttaraḥ

|| 27||
mahābhiṣagiti

sakalakleśapratipakṣaprakṛtiprabhāsvaramahāsukhākāra- sākṣātkāritvāt kleśavyādhīnāṃ rāgavirāgādilakṣaṇānāṃ mahāripurhantā

||28||
trailokyeti
trailokyaṃ kāyavākcittekatvaṃ tasya mahāsukhālaṃkṛtatvena tilaka- rūpatvāt ' trailokya tilakaḥ
svādhiṣṭhāna rūpatvātsundaraḥ kāntaḥ
advayasvarūpaparamārtha- siddhiśrīyogāt śrīmān
svasaṃvedya prakāśakamahāsukhatayā gagane nakṣatramaṇḍalavat spharaṇānnakṣatramaṇḍalaḥ
ārṣatvāt puṃstvam
athavā uṣṇīṣe catasro lalāṭe ṣoḍaśa etā viśati nāḍyaḥ śleṣmaghātuprakopikāḥ
kaṇṭhe dvātriṃśad hṛdi aṣṭa etā catvāriṃ- ga. bhāskara
kha. da prāptastādā ka. kha. da. kāraṇānya

ka. ga. advayarūpa
āryamañjuśrī nāmasaṅgītiḥ śannāḍhyaḥ pittadhātuprako pikāḥ
nābhau catuḥṣaṣṭināḍyaḥ, guhyakamalasya bāhyaparimaṇḍale ṣoḍaśa
etā aśītināṭyo vāyudhātuprako pikāḥ
'asyaiva madhyaparimaṇḍale daśa, garbhaparimaṇḍale ṣaṭ, yathāsaṃkhyaṃ sannipātaprakopikāḥ
ṣaṭcakranirodhasvabhāvaḥ
evaṃ ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikaśatanācyo bālānāṃ mṛtyudāyikāḥ
yogināṃ sukhadāyikāḥ
ṣaṭkula- nāḍībhiḥ sārddhadvāṣaṣṭyadhikaśatanāḍyaḥ
etāsu ca nāḍīṣu pratyekanāḍyo daśavāyupracāreṇa skandhadhātudaśasva 'bhāvena daśadhā bhavanti
evaṃ sarvaṃ daśaguṇitā viṃśatyadhikaṣoḍaśarāta- saṃkhyā bhavanti
saptaviṃśatinakṣatraghaṭikāvāhinyaḥ
etannirodhāt śrīmānnakṣatramaṇḍala iti
daśadiśā vyomaprabhāsvaratvaṃ tasya sphuṭībhāvena paryantaḥ paramārthabindurūpaḥ, sa eva dharmadhvaja mahocchrayo brahmasthāna gatatvena yasya sa tathā

jagacchatraka vipulo maitrī karuṇamaṇḍalaḥ
padma nṛtyeśvaraḥ śrīmān ratnacchatro mahāvibhuḥ
|| 29||
jagacchatreti
sakalakleśatāpāpahāritvena kāyavākcittai kalolībhāvarūpa- jagadevaikamadvitīyaṃ cchatram
avicchinnamahāsukhādhārārpitacaṇḍālī kanakadaṇḍoddhṛtatvena uṣṇīṣagataṃ romakūpaparyantagatatvena vipulaṃ vistīrṇaṃ yasya sa tathā
jyotiḥ svabhāvena maitrī, bodhicittatvena karuṇā tayomaṃṇḍaṃ sāraṃ 'lātoti sa tathā
avācyasukhabodhi- cittatvātpadamanarteśvara: vaṣtra' padmollāsaprabhurityarthaḥ, ata eva śrīmān
caṇḍālījvalana- prayogeṇa vajramaṇiśikhare sthitatvāt sakalavikalpa tāpoparodhāt sarvāṅga śītala- karaṇācchatramiva cchatro ratnacchatraḥ
sarvabhāvarūpatvānmahāvibhuḥ
|| 29 ||

||30|
sarvabuddheti
sarvabuddhānāṃ vairocanādīnāṃ mahārājaḥ vajrasattvo bodhicitta- svabhāvatvāt
sarvabuddhānāmātmā sarvadharmaśūnyatā sa eva bhāvaḥ sadrūpastaddharatīti sa tathā, skandhadhātvādirūpasarva tathāgata hṛdayavihāritvāt
sarvabuddhānāṃ mahāyogaḥ parama- sukhākārasamādhirūpaḥ
sarvabuddhānāṃ skandha dīnāṃ ekamekatvaṃ sukhena śāsti sampādayatīti tathā
|| 30 ||
amṛta kaṇikāsahitā vajraratnābhiṣekaśrīḥ sarvaratnādhipeśvaraḥ
sarvalokeśvarapatiḥ sarvavajradharāṣipaḥ
|| 31 ||
vajraratneti
'vajraratno'bhiṣekaḥ sahajānandasaukhyaṃ sa evaṃ dhīstadrūpatvād bhagavānapi tathā sarvaratnādhipasya buddhasyeśvaraḥ
śirasi dhṛtatvāt sarvalokānāṃ buddhabodhisattvānāṃ īśvaro vajrasattvaḥ sa eva patiḥ sarvabhāvamayatvāt
kāyādibindutrayadhāriṇaḥ sarva- vajragharāsteṣāmadhipatiḥ śūnyatākaruṇābhinnacaturthabinduḥ
|| 31 ||
sarvabuddha mahā cittaḥ sarvaṃbuddha mahākāya : sarva buddhamanogatiḥ | sarvabuddha sarasvatī ||32||
sarvabuddheti |
sarvabuddhānāmapratighacittatvena mahācittarūpatvāttathā |
sarvabuddhānāṃ manasi acittacitte gatirlayastādātmyaṃ yasya sa tathā tathoktaḥ |
dharmadhāturūpajñāna- kāyatvena sarva dharmavyāpanāt sarvabuddhamahākāyaḥ |
sarvabuddhānāṃ kāyavākcittānāṃ mahā- sukhāmṛtapravāhanimajjanādhāramahāsukhapūrṇācintyana dīrūpatvāt sarvabuddhasarasyatiḥ ( tī ) |
ārṣatvālliṅgavyatyayaḥ sarvabuddhamayī sarasvatī acintya sukhā nadī yasya sa tathā hrasvatvam || 32 ||
bajrasūrya mahāloko virāgādimahārāgo vajrenduvimalaprabhaḥ | viśvavarṇojjvalaprabhaḥ ||33||
vajrasūryeti |
vajrasūryasya jñānamaṇḍalasya mahānālokaḥ sakalatraidhātukāvabhāso maṇivaraṭakāntaḥ sthitacittakṣaṇalakṣaṇo yasya sa tathā |
uktañca - vajrasūryañca yaccittaṃ maṇyantargatamīkṣayet | jñānamaṇḍalākāreṇa dhātukāvabhāsataḥ || iti |
anābhogenādvayacitta iti yāvat |
vandhujñānenduḥ sucandraḥ tasya vimalā sakalavikalpāpagatā prabhā trailokyāvabhāso yasya sa tathā |
candrasūryarāhnagnirajaḥ- śukracittajñānekayogaḥ padadvayenokta ityarthaḥ |
virāgasya viraktilakṣaṇasyādirmahārāgaḥ sahajānandātmakaścaturthastādātmyena yasya sa tathā |
viśvavarṇasya nānāvarṇasyojjvalā sakalavikalpātaṃkavidhamanī prabhā yasya sa tathā |
idaṃ ca guruvaktram |
uktañca- āryamañjuśrīnāmasaṅgītiḥ dṛṣṭe bimbe prakuryāt | pratidinasamaye prāṇavāyonirodhaṃ yāvadve bhrāmyamāṇaṃ svatanuparivṛtaṃ dṛśyate raśmicakram | ṣaṇmāsaiḥ sparśahīnaṃ vrajati samasukhaṃ mārgacittaṃ yatīnāṃ | rāgārāgāntagādyaṃ kṣaṇamapi ca vibho varddhate śvāsasaṃkhyam || odrā jvālāntarāle viramasahajayorjñānavijñānamadhye :: nidrā ghūrmābhisandhau kuliśakamalayoryatsukhaṃ dvandvayogāt | vṛddhi tasya prakuryād guruniyamavaśād barddhate nātra citraṃ .
hatvā 'kleśāṃśca mārān viśati jinapati varṣayogātsuyogī || iti |

(kā ta uktañca vimalaprabhāyām rāgo bodhicittasya śuklapakṣasya pañcadaśakalāḥ virāgaḥ kṛṣṇapakṣasya : pañcadaśakalāḥ | tanmadhye sahajānandaḥ ṣoḍaśīkalā sarvadhātūnāṃ ṣoḍaśokalāḥ | sarvadhātūnāṃ samāhāraḥ samājaḥ saṃvara iti || 33 ||

sambuddheti |

kāyavā cittajñānabindughṛg vā vajraparyaṃkataścittaṃ ( vajrasūyaṃ ca yaccittaṃ ) maṇyantargatamīkṣayed” iti vacanāt |
buddhaṃ nirvikalpajñānaṃ tadeva kleśavāsanāmalānanulipta - svānmahāsukhākāratvācca padmaṃ mahāmudrā tad udbhavatīti buddhapadmodbhavaḥ |
ata eva śrīmān |

viśvamāyetiḥ|
vaqāropari rajo viśuddha vaśabdarūpārddhacandropari bindusvarūpaṃ sukhaṃ bodhicittamanāhatarūpanādānvitaṃ viśvamāyāṃ pañcakāmopabhogādirūpāṃ dhārayatīti tathā |

duḥkhaccheda mahāyāno vajradharma mahāyudhaḥ | jina jig vajra gāmbhīryo vajra buddhiryathārthavit || 36 ||
duḥkhacchedeti |
duḥkhaṃ cchinattīti tathā taṃ mahāyānaṃ mārgasatyaṃ yasya sa tathā |
vajradharmo jñānadharmaḥ |
sa eva mahāyudhaṃ yasya sa tathā |
jinān akṣobhyādīn janayatīti nairuktyā jina jig, vimalanirābhāsa cittāvabodharūpa ityarthaḥ |
vajrasya samyagjñānasya gāmbhīryaṃ grāhyādivikalparahitā prajñā svabhāvo yasya sa tathā |
vajramārgācyutatvena vajrabuddhiḥ |
phalasvarūpasphuṭībhāvād yathārthavit || 36 ||
sarvapāramitāpūrī viśuddhadharmaṃ nairātmyaḥ sarva bhūmivibhūṣaṇaḥ | samyagjñānenduhṛtprabhaḥ || 37||
dānādipāramitāḥ pūrayatīti sarvapāramitāpūrī |
pūrvokta bhūmimaṇḍala tvāt sarvabhūmivibhūṣaṇaḥ |
viśuddhadharmasya citta prativedhalakṣaṇasya nerātmyaṃ nirābhāsīkaraṇa- dharmatārūpaṃ prakṛtiprabhāsvaram tena samutpannaḥ samyagjñānenduḥ sahajānandaścandrastasya hutprabhā mahāsukhākārā yasya sa tathoktaḥ || 37 ||
māyājāla mahodyoga : sarvatantrādhipaḥ paraḥ | aśeṣavajraparyaṅko niḥśeṣajñānakāyadhṛk ||38||
( maho ) yogo yasya sa tathā |
sarvatantrāṇāṃ yogādimahātantrāṇāṃ tasyaiva pratipādyatvāt sarva tantrādhipaḥ sa ca paraḥ |
prāṇāpānoparodhādadvayībhūtāśeṣasamādhivajramayaḥ paryaṅko yasyāsāvaśeṣavajjraparyaṅkaḥ |
niḥśeṣāṇāṃ buddhānāṃ jñānakāryaṃ paramādvayībhūtaṃ dhārayatīti niḥśeṣajñānakāyadhṛk || 38 ||
āryamañjuśrīnāmasaṅgītiḥ samantabhadraḥ sumatiḥ kṣitigarbho jagaddhṛtiḥ | sarvabuddhamahāgarbho viśvanirmāṇacakradhṛk || 39||
samanteti |
dhūmādinimittasphuṭībhāvena samantato bhadraṃ sahajānandaṃ jñānaṃ yasya sa tathā |
sarvavikalpābhāvena sambodhiprāptatvena śūnyatākāraśobhanā matiryasya sa tathā |
kṣitiśabdena pañcabhūtopalakṣaṇāt kṣitigarbho hetuḥ kṣitigarbhaḥ sahajānandabindu:, ata eva jagaddhṛtiḥ sakalajagadādhāraḥ |
sarvabuddhānāṃ tryadhvavattināṃ ṣaḍaṅgayogabhāvakānāṃ utpatti- hetutvāt sarvabuddhamahāgarbhaḥ sahajabodhicittavantraḥ |
laukika lokottaradevatāspharaṇaṃ viśvanirmāṇaṃ tadeva cakraṃ maṇḍalaṃ taddhārayatīti viśva nirmāṇacakradhṛk śukrarajodvayekalolī- bhūtabāhyādhyātmika kāyavākcittadhara ityarthaḥ || 39 ||
sarvaṃbhāvasvabhāvāgryaḥ sarvabhāvasvabhāvadhṛk | anutpādadharmā viśvārthaḥ sarvadharmasvabhāvadhṛk || 40 ||
· sarvabhāveti |
sarveṣāṃ bhāvānāṃ svabhāvaḥ śuddhatathāgatajñānakāyaḥ sa cāso agrayaśca sarvabhāvasvabhāvācyaḥ |
dharmasambhogādvayī bhūta nirmāṇalakṣaṇānāṃ sarvabhāvānāṃ svabhāvaṃ nairātmyaṃ dhārayatīti sarvabhāvasvabhāvaghṛk|
'anutpādo niḥsvabhāvastattvaṃ dharmo yasya sa tathā |
viśveṣāmarthaḥ sādhyo'bhilaṣaṇīyaḥ |
sarvadharmāṇāṃ skandhadhātvāyatanādīnāṃ svabhāvaṃ yathābhūtaparijñānaṃ dhārayatīti sarvadharmasvabhāvadhṛk || 40 ||
ekakṣaṇamahāprājñaḥ sarvadharmāva bodhadhṛk | sarvadharmābhisamayo bhūtāntamuniragradhīḥ ||41||
ekakṣaṇeti
eko'dvitīyaḥ kṣaṇasturyātītaścaturānandaikamūttiḥ sahajasambodhi- lakṣaṇaḥ tatra mahāprājñaḥ
ṣaṭśatādhike kaviṃśatisahasraśvāsa mahāsukhaikalayabuddhimān ityarthaḥ
anenāpi caturthā nandaśrormañjuśrīḥ bhagavān
uktañca- • dambholibījaśrutadhautaśuddhaṃ pāthojñabhūtāṅkurabhūtapuṣṭi
turīyaśasyaṃ paripākameti sphuṭañcaturthaṃ viduṣo'pi gūḍham || iti
sarvadharmāṇāṃ sarvaśvāsāḥ nāmavabodhasukha kalayāvagamaṃ dhārayatīti sarvadharmāva- bodhaka
sarvadharmāḥ sattvarajastamāṃsi abhisamīyante sākṣāt kriyante sahajacandro- ityadhikam
amṛtakaṇikāsahitā bhūtatvena yena sa tathā
bhūtaṃ satyaṃ tathatā tasyāntaḥprakarṣaḥ phalāvasthā tasya yathāvan- mananād bhūtāntamuniḥ
agradhīrva jāgrasukhāva 'bodhanāt || 41 ||
stimitaḥ suprasannātmā samyak saṃbuddhabodhidhṛk
pratyakṣaḥ sarvabuddhānāṃ jñānāciḥ suprabhāsvaraḥ || 42 || iti
`pratyavekṣaṇajñānagāthāḥ dvācatvāriṃśat
stimiteti
sahajānandasukhāpyāyamānatvāt stimitaḥ, samādhisphuṭībhāvenā- calatvāt suṣṭhu prasanna ātmā svarūpaṃ yasya sa tathā
vañcaratnāntargataṃ cittaṃ samyak saṃbuddhaḥ tasya bodhisukhajñānaṃ taddhārayatīti tathā
svasaṃvedya sahajajñānapriyatayā sarvabuddhānāṃ tryadhvavattināṃ pratyakṣaḥ
jñānāciṣā prakṛtiprabhāsvaranirālambajñānena suprabhāsvaro'na- ntānantabuddhakṣetraprakāśakaḥ ||42|| iti
uṣṇīṣādimaṇi śikharāntasthānasaptakeṣu nirāvaraṇīkṛtaṣaṭskandha- dhātvindriyaviṣaya karmendriyakriyāṣaṭka viśuddha cādvācatvāriṃśa- cchalokyā' pratyavekṣaṇājñānavyākhyā || 8 ||
samatājñānam idānīmiṣṭārthasādhaka ityādinā nirāvaraṇavedanāskandharatnasambhavarūpeṇa samatā- jñānamukhena tadeva sahajajñānamāha- 'iṣṭārthasādhakaḥ paraḥ sarvasattvottamo nāthaḥ sarvāpāya viśodhakaḥ
sarva sattva pramocakaḥ ||1||
iṣṭārthasādhaketi
sarvāśāparipūrakaparamākṣarajñānarūpatvādiṣṭa śvāsāvarthaśceti iṣṭārthaḥ, tasya sāghako niṣpādakaḥ vajra padmānubhūtabindu trayātītatvāt paraḥ
sarvāpāyasya saṃsāravāsana (nā) yā viśodhako mahāsukhaikakarttā
sarvasattvānāṃ kāyavāk- cittānāmuttamo jñānakāyaḥ, ata eva nātho anāthānāṃ mahāmudrā padmaprakāśakatvena sarvasattvānāṃ pramocakaḥ ||1||
kleśasaṃgrāmaśūraikaḥ ajñāna ripudarpahā
ghīśṭaṅgāradharaḥ śrīmān vīrabībhatsa rūpadhṛk || 2 ||
kleśasaṃgrāmeti
sarvasattvacaritakleśānāṃ samyagjñānaśastreṇa nikṛntanāt
kleśasaṃgrāme eko'dvitīyaḥ śūraḥ
ārṣatvādanyathāśabdavinyāsaḥ
ajñānaṃ avidyā seva ripuracyutiḥ duḥkhadāyitvāt tasya darpo varhivikṣepa:, tannirābhāsīkaraṇāvajñānaripu- darpahā
cittamātrasyāpi nirasanenāpagatakleśopakleśatvād dhīḥ

uṣṇīṣāducchalitabodhicittadhārayā sarvāṅgā- pyāyanena laukikalokottarasampattimān śrīmān
upāyasya karuṇāṅgatvena vīrarasatvaṃ prajñāyāḥ śūnyatāṅgatvena bībhatsarasatvaṃ tadvedharūpaṃ mahāsukhavīrabībhatsarūpaṃ taddharatīti tathā || 2 ||

athavā mahī śūnyatā śukrabījadhāraṇāt tasyā maṇḍaḥ sāra iti yojyam
brahmāṇḍaśikharaṃ brahmarandhaṃ tadākrānto yo'sau pādāṅguṣṭhanakho: vighṛti krameṇoṣṇīṣagatavajjrasaṃbandhi cittaṃ tatra sthito'dvaya yogena ekaṃ padaṃ vajramaṇo rajo'rke uṣṇīṣaśukre śaśini dvitīyam

avidyā nirodhāya vajramaṇāvapratiṣṭhita nirvāṇabhūmī rajo'rke mahomāṇḍalatale jñānaghātī ekaṃ padaṃ dakṣiṇamityekapādatalākrāntaḥ

bodhyaṅgeti
vairocanādiṣaṭtathāgatānāṃ mahāsukhena sahaikalolībhūtatvena saptāvarta - rūpatvād bhuktapītādyannapānādiṣaḍrasapākato mūrcchājāraṇādinā rasarudhiramāṃsacarmanāḍī- asthimajjārūpatayā vā saptāvartarūpatvāt saptabindubodhyaṅgakusumānāmāmodo mahā- sukhollāso yasya sa tathā
saptāvarttantu khādyate 'cyutī kriyata ityayamevārthaḥ
uktañca– āryamañjuśrīnāmasaṅgītiḥ skandhadhātvādike dagdhe pañcamaṇḍalavāhake
viṣayendriyaniruddhe cānandādye samucchite ||
srāvati bindukānindo 'haṃkāro mūrdhnisaṃsthitaḥ
lalāṭe candrataḥ sūrye kaṇṭhād dravo tato hṛdi ||
nābhau caṇḍālikāviṣṭaṃ guhya cakre tato gataṃ
sampāto guhyacakre'sminkathito'yaṃ mahāpaśuḥ ||
trikādhastrināḍīnāṃ patracandraṃ pradarśitam
saptajanmā paśuścaiva khecarīsiddhidāyakaḥ ||
mūcchito harate vyādhi 'harati skandhadhātukam
dadāti cakravattitvaṃ viṣayendriyamāritaḥ dadātyeṣo'ṇimādiguṇairyutaḥ
khecaratvaṃ kṣipraṃ dadāti buddhatvaṃ ja raṇādyeranu sāritaḥ ||
'bāhyāmṛto yathā baddhastathājñānaraso'pyayam
dadāti vipulāṃ siddhi sarvaduḥkhāpahāriṇīm ||
evaṃ brahmā paśurviṣṇurguhyacakre nipātitaḥ
dadāti vipulaṃ hyāyuryāvadā hṛdi saṃplavam ||
urdhva rāhuḥ paśuḥ proktaścandrasūryau mahāpaśūḥ
pātitā brahmarandhreṇa bhuktimuktiphalapradāḥ ||
raktasnāyustathā majjā pātanīyaḥ prayatnataḥ
ete ca paśavo jñeyā nānyabāhye'stu dehinām ||
sapta janmāstu sarveṣāṃ paśūnāṃ parameśvaraḥ
saptajanmā yathā dehe tathā vakṣyāmi tacchṛṇu ||
annapānarasaṃ sarvaṃ bhuktaṃ pītañca ṣaḍvidham
pākato rasatāṃ yāti prathamaṃ janma tasya tat ||
paścād rudhiratāṃ yāti dvitīyaṃ bhavati sphuṭam ||
tṛtīyaṃ māṃsatāṃ yāti caturthaṃ carmatāṃ vrajet ||
amṛtakaṇikāsahitā nāḍītvaṃ pañcame yāti 'asthitāṃ yāti ṣaṣṭhame saptame majjatāṃ yāti saptāvattaṃ bhavediti ||
tathā
tathatā dharmodayā avadhūtī tadgatatvena sarvaguṇodayāddaśavidha- prāṇādivāyunirodhena daśabhūmisamantaprabhāparyantaphalabhūmi prāptaḥ tathāgataḥ
sa eva guṇovadhiḥ sarvabhūmyāśrayatvena tattara sattvārthakaraṇāt
antargatākāśamaṇḍalatvena vāma- dakṣiṇanāsāpuṭayoḥ prāṇavāhamaṇḍalānyaṣṭau teṣāṃ mārgo madhyamāpraveśastannayaṃ tadupadeśa - gurūpadiṣṭapratyāhārādinā vettīti tathā
samayakasaṃbuddhaḥ svayaṃbhūḥ jñānī tasya mārgo dharmaghātujñānaṃ tatsākṣātkārāttathā || 10 ||
sarvasattvamahāsaṅgo niḥsaṅgo gaganopamaḥ sarvasattvamano jātaḥ sarvasattvamanojavaḥ ||11||

śūnyatā svabhāvena nissaṅgaḥ
vikalpa' lepābhāvena gaganopamaḥ
prakṛtiprabhāsvaratvamanasikāreṇa sarvasattvānāṃ manasi - jātatvāttathā
sarva sattveṣvadhimuktivaśena vajradharādipratibhāsarūpatvāt sarvaṃ sattva- manojavah || 11 ||
sarvasattvendriyārthajñaḥ sarvasattvamanoharaḥ pañcaskandhārthatattvajñaḥ pañcaskandhaviśuddhadhṛk ||12||
sarvasattvendriyeti
sarvasattvānāṃ mṛdumadhyādhimātrarūpāṇīndriyāṇi teṣāmarthaḥ 'saprapañcaniṣprapañcamavyāhata mahāsukhasamādhinā jānātīti tathā
sarvasattvānāṃ manāṃsi vikalpacittāni hati tāni niḥsvabhāvīkaroti tīti sarvasattvamanoharaḥ
pañcaskandhārthatvaṃ pāramārthikavatreṇa sarvatathāgatavyūhasya tathatāyāṃ praveśastajjñānāttathā
uktañca - rūpaskandhagatādarśa bhūdhātunayanendriyam
rūpaṃ ca pañcamaṃ yāti krodhamaitreyasaṃyutam ||
ityādi
prabhāsvarotthānā tu prakṛtiprabhāsvaratāṃ pañcaskandhārthaṃ viśuddhaṃ dharatīti pañcaskandhaviśuddhadhak || 12 ||
āryamañjuśrī nāmasaṅgītiḥ sarva' niryāṇakoṭisthaḥ sarvaniryāṇa mārgasthaḥ sarvaniryāṇakovidaḥ
sarvaniryāṇadeśakaḥ
|| 13 ||
sarvaniryāṇeti
sarvaniryāṇakoṭau yuganaddhasamādhau sthitatvāttathā
avācya- nairātmyaṃ sukhavedakatvāt sarvaniryāṇakovidaḥ
mahāsukharatnatvena niḥsaṅgatve'pi mahākaruṇābalena śrāvakādimārge sthitatvāt sarvaniryāṇamāgaṃsthaḥ
mahāsukhakāya- dharmeṇa dharmadhātuḥ sarvaniryāṇaṃ tasya deśa' ko'vikalpena prakāśakaḥ
|| 13 ||
dvādaśāṅgabhavotkhāto catuḥ satyanayākāraḥ dvādaśākāraśuddhadhṛk
aṣṭajñānāvabodhadhṛk
|| 14||
dvādaśāṅgeti
vā
dvādaśāṅgabhavotkhātaḥ
uktañca- parapīṭheti prajñoktā ātmapīṭhamupāyakam
anayoradvayībhāvo yogapīṭha iti smṛtam
// tatsvapīṭhaṃ tadutpannaṃ tadrahitaṃ ca yad bhavet
sahajeti samākhyātaṃ vākpathātītagocaram
// iti
pīṭhaṃ strīguhyapadmaṃ prabhavati samaye vajramevopapīṭhaṃ kṣetraṃ chandohamelāpaka citibhuvanaṃ tadvadevaṃ samastam
poṭhaṃ vāmāṅgapūrvaṃ hyaparamapi tathā dakṣiṇañcopapīṭham evaṃ kṣetrādi sarvaṃ karacaraṇagataṃ cāṅgulījānuṃ sīmnaḥ
// iti
grāhyagrāhakākāraviviktadvādaśāyatanadhāritvād dvādaśākāraśuddhadhṛk
prākṛta- kalpitasukhaṃ duḥkham
turyālakṣaṇaḥ samudayaḥ
jñānamaṇḍalāntargatakāyavākcittaprakṛti- nirodho [ nirodhasatyam ]
nirālambasvacittapratibhāsajagadbuddha bimbadarśanaṃ mārgaḥ
etatsarvādvayatvamākāro yasya sa tathā
vicitrādicatuṣṭaya niḥspandādicatuṣṭaya mahārati- jñānāṣṭakadhāraṇādaṣṭajñānāvabodhaghṛk
|| 14 ||
amṛta kaṇikāsahitā dvādaśākārasatyārthaḥ ṣoḍaśākāratattvavit
viśatyākārasaṃbodhivibuddhaḥ sarvavit paraḥ
|| 15 ||
dvādaśākāreti niḥspa (sya) ndavipākapuruṣa (ṣā) kāravaimalyakṣaṇānāṃ pratyekaṃ traiguṇyād dvādaśākārā dvādaśabhūmayaḥ saṃvṛtiparamārthasatyābhedena tadyogād dvādaśākāra- satyārthaḥ
tatra kāyavākcittavivekaniḥspa (sya) ndabhedena prathamaṃ trikaṃ nirmāṇakāyasya
bhāsa - pratibhāsa-prakṛtinirābhāsavipākabhedena dvitīyaṃ trikaṃ saṃbhogakāyasya
cittacaita- sikāvidyāpuruṣa (ṣā) kāra[vaimalyavipāka ] bhedena tṛtīyaṃ trikaṃ dharmakāyasya
ānanda- dvayaviramasahajānandavaimalyabhedena caturthatrikaṃ jñānakāyasyeti
prāṇakṣayeṇa rajo nirodhādvā nirodhitameṣavṛṣādidvādaśarāśitvena vā dvādaśabhūmilābhād dvādaśākā rasatyārthaḥ
ṣoḍaśānanda kalāṃ śukranirodhena ṣoḍaśākāratattvaṃ vettīti tathā
sāṃsārikaṣoḍaśakalā- [kṣayato']kṣaratvāt ṣoḍaśākāratattvavit
ṣaḍaṅgabhāvanayā viśuddhāvadhūtīviśuddhatvena pañcaskandha-pañcadhātu-pañcendriya pañcāyatanaviśuddhayāka rāvabodhād viśatyākāra - sambodhiḥ
avidyānirodhād vibuddhaḥ
sarvabuddhamaya bhāvagrāmāvabodhāt sarvavit
|| 15 ||
ameyabuddhanirmāṇa kāya koṭivibhāvaka : sarvakṣaṇābhisamayaḥ sarvacittakṣaṇārthaṃvit
||16||
ameyeti
sarvanirvāṇakalolobhūtāprameyabuddhanirmāṇarūpabhāvagrāmasya śvāsasamūhasya mahāsukhatvenaikakṣaṇe vibhāvanāttathā
anena madhya nimittamuktam
pratiśvāsaṃ sarvaśvāsānāṃ mahāsukhena bodhāt sarvakṣaṇābhisamayaḥ
sarvasattvānāṃ cittakṣaṇānāṃ ca mahāsukhatvenaiva vettīti tathā
|| 16 ||
nānāyānanayo pāyajagadarthavibhāvaka : yānatritayaniryāta ekayānaphale sthitaḥ
|| 17 ||
nānāyāneti
nānāyānanayopāyena caturaṅga - ṣaḍaṅgabinduyogādinā jagadayaṃ mahāsukhatvena vibhāvayati sphārayatīti tathā
jñānakāyatvena kāyavākcittaniryā- tatvād yānatri tayaniryātaḥ
ekamadvitīyaṃ yānaṃ phalaṃ haṃkārāstatra sthitaḥ
|| 17 ||
kleśadhātu viśuddhātmā oghodadhisamuttīrṇo karmadhātukṣayaṅkaraḥ
yogakāntāraniḥsṛtaḥ
||18||
āryamanjuśrīnāmasaṅgītiḥ - kleśadhātviti
avidyāyā vināśitvāt kleśadhātūnāṃ viśuddhātmā yasya sa tathā
te ca kleśadhātavo'ṣṭādaśa asthi- carma- māṃsa-rakta-snāyu-majjā - medaḥ -śukra- śleṣma-viṇmūtra- kheṭa-'siṃhā (ghā) ṇa-yakṛta- plīhā-piśita-mala-romākhyāḥ
pāpapuṇya vikalpā- bhāvāt karmaghātukṣayaṃkaraḥ
sarvaprakṛtinirodhena jñānapāragatvād oghodadhisa- sutīrṇaḥ
tāśca prakṛtayo virāgo madhyamavirāgo'tivirāgo yanmano gatāgatam śoko madhyamaśoko'tiśokaḥ saumyaṃ vikalpo bhītaṃ tṛṣṇā madhyamatṛṣṇā * atitṛṣṇā' upādānaṃ niḥśubhra kṣuttṛṣā vedanā samavedanā ativedanā vidavid dhāraṇā padaṃ pratyavekṣaṇaṃ lajjā kāruṇyaṃ sneho madhyamasneho'tisnehaścakitaṃ sañcayo mātsaryamiti trayastriṃśat kṣaṇā: prajñājñānasya
rāgo raktaṃ tuṣṭaṃ madhyamatuṣṭaṃ atituṣṭaṃ harṣaṇaṃ prāmodyaṃ vismayo hasitamā 'hlādanamāliṅganaṃ cumbanaṃ 'cūṣaṇaṃ stheyaṃ vīryaṃ mānaḥ karaṇaṃ haraṇaṃ balamutsāhaḥ sāhasaṃ madhyamasāhasamuttamasāhasaṃ raudraṃ vilāso vairaṃ śubhaṃ vāksphuṭaṃ satyam 'asatyaṃ niścayo nirupādānaṃ dāhatvaṃ codanaṃ śauyaṃ alajjā ghūrtatvaṃ duṣṭatvaṃ śaṭha- koṭilyamiti catvāriṃśat kṣaṇā upāyajñānasya
madhyarāgo vismṛtirbhrāntistūṣṇīṃ kheda ālasyaṃ dhandhatvamiti saptakṣaṇā ālokopalabdhijñānasya
etāśca divārātripravarttanā- bhedena ṣaṣṭyuttaraśataṃ sambhavanti
sakalavikalpabhakṣādyogakāntāranissṛtayogī jñāna- samāpattimātraṃ kāntāraṃ duravabodhāt
|| 18 ||
kleśopakleśa saṃkleśa suprahīṇasavāsanaḥ prajñopāyamahākaruṇā-amogha jagadarthaṃkṛt
||19||
kleśopakleśeti
kleśopakleśasaṃkleśānāṃ avidyādīnāṃ suprahoṇā suṣṭhuvāsanā yasya sa tathā
ṣaḍaṅgayogena sāghitā mahāmudrā prajñā tadadvayībhūtā mahākaruṇā tayā 'amoghaṃ abandhyamavyāhatasukhaṃ jagadarthaṅkarotīti tathā
|| 19||
sarva saṃjñā prahīṇārtho vijñānārtho nirodhadhṛk
sarvaṃ sattvamanoviṣayaḥ sarvasattvamanogatiḥ
||20||
sarvasaṃjñeti
samāpattyādisaṃjñayā abhāvāt sarvasaṃjñāprahīṇārthaḥ
vigataṃ jñānaṃ grahaṇamartho bāhyañca yasya sa tathā
ata eva nirāsravadharmatā mātratvānnirodhaghṛk
sarvasattvānāṃ manasi avikalpayogena saṃbhūtatvāt sarvasattvamanoviṣaya aviṣayo agocaro vā, ata eva sarvasatvamanogatiḥ

siddhānteti |

pañcaskandhārthaṃ strikālaḥ sarvakṣaṇavibhāvakaḥ |

pañcaskandheti |

nityatvena trikālaḥ |

'anaṅgakāya: kāyāśyaḥ kāya koṭivibhāvakaḥ |

iti samatā jñānagāthāścaturviṃśatiḥ |

tmakottaro |
mChog Tu Mi hGyur Pahi ( paramākṣara ) |
amṛta kaṇikāsahitā: jñānaskandhavijñānaskandhajñānadhātvākāśadhātumanaḥśrotraśabdadharmadhātudivyendriyabhaga- mūtrasrāvaśukracyutirūpāṇāṃ nirāvaraṇatā pratyekaṃ svasva 'viṣayagrahaṇa vivarjitatvaṃ samarasa- mekalolībhūtatvaṃ para (prathamākṣaraśūnyaṃ yogisvasaṃvedyam, na sarvābhāvalakṣaṇam |
tadevāha - tamā- kāraśūnyaṃ rekhāmātramanuccārya kartṛkākāraṃ madhye, saṃskāraskandhavāyughāṃtughrāṇasparśa- vāgindriyaviṭsrāvāṇāmekalolībhūtatvaṃ madhyānāhatacihnāt pūrveṇa, ikāraśūnyaṃ daṇḍākāraṃ dvitīyākṣaraśūnyam |
vedanāskandhatejodhātucakṣurasapāṇigatīnāṃ nirāvaraṇatā madhyā- nāhatacihnāddakṣiṇena kāraśūnyaṃ bindudvayaṃ tṛtīyākṣaraśūnyam |
saṃjñāskandhatoyadhātu jihvā- rūpapāde ndriyādānānāṃ nirāvaraṇatā madhyānāhatacihnādvāmena, bindurūpamekamanuccāryamūkāra- śūnyaṃ caturthākṣaramahāśūnyam |
rūpaskandhapṛthivīdhātukāyendriyagandhapāyvālāpānāṃ samarasa- tvamekalolībhūtatvaṃ, madhyānāhatacihnāt paścimena, tad halākṛtiḥ ekāraśūnyaṃ pañcākṣara- śūnyam |
evaṃ vaṃkāraḥ pañcākṣaro mahāśūnyo nirālamba karuṇātmakaḥ, paramāṇudharmatātītaḥ, pratisenātulyo yogigamyaḥ, tadādhārabhūta ekāro dharmodayarūpaḥ sarvākāraśūnyatāsvabhāvo binduśūnyaḥ ṣaḍakṣaraḥ |
tathāhi - vijñānaskandha ākāśaghātuśrotradharmadhātubhagaśukracyutīnāṃ nirāvaraṇaśūnyatā |
pūrvoktā madhyānāhatacihnasyordhve kavargātmakaṃ kakāravyañjanamanuccārya prathamaṃ binduśūnyam |
saṃskāraskandhavāyughātughrāṇendriyasparśavāg viṭsrāvāṇāṃ nirāvaraṇa- sarvākāraśūnyatā |
pūrvoktacihnasya pūrve cavargātmakaṃ cakāravyañjanamanuccāyaṃ [ dvitīyaṃ ] binduśūnyam |
vedanāskandhatejodhātucakṣurasapāṇīndriyagatīnāṃ sarvākāranirāvaraṇa- śūnyatā |
dakṣiṇacihnasya dakṣiṇe ṭavargātmakaṃ ṭakāravyañjanamanuccāryaṃ tṛtīyaṃ bindu- śūnyam |
saṃjñāskanghatoyadhātujihvārūpapādendriyādānānāṃ nirāvaraṇaśūnyatā |
uttaracihna- syottare pavargātmakaṃ pakāravyañjanamanuccāryaṃ caturthabinduśūnyam |
rūpaskandhapṛthivīghātu- kāyendriyagandhapāyvālāpānāṃ nirāvaraṇaśūnyatā |
paścimacihnasya paścime tavargātmakaṃ takāravyañjanamanuccāryaṃ pañcamaṃ binduśūnyam |
jñānaskandhajñānadhātumanaḥśabdadivyendriya- mūtrasrāvāṇāṃ sarvākāranirāvaraṇaśūnyatā |
madhyānāhatacihnasyādhastāt śavargātmakaṃ śakāravyañjanamanuccāyaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ binduśūnyamiti |
evaṃ binduśūnyaḥ ṣaḍakṣaro dharmodayaḥ |
kuliśadhara ekāraḥ śūnyatā sālambā pratisenāsvarūpiṇīti |
etena pañcākṣaro mahā- śūnyaḥ |
svarasamūhaḥ candra ityucyate |
binduśūnyaḥ ṣaḍakṣaro vyañjanasamūho rajaḥ sūrya ityucyate |
atra ca śukraṃ candro vaṃkāro vajraṃ, rajaḥ sūrya ekāraḥ padmam, anayorvajra- padmayorekatvaṃ vastra sattva iti |
vaṣtraṃ paramasukhaṃ, jñānaṃ śukraṃ, sattvaḥ sarvākāra prajñābimbaṃ jñeyamiti |
jñānavijñānādhiṣṭhitaṃ nirāvaraṇamekalolī bhūtatvaṃ jagadarthakārī vastrasattva iti |
etacca vimalaprabhāyām uktaṃ jñeyamiti || 2 ||

sarvākāreti |
sarvākāranirākāraheturuktaḥ |
pratyāhāreṇa yo dṛṣṭo bhāvo ghaṭādikaḥ
pratisenātulyapratibhāsaḥ sarvākāraḥ
kalpanāpoḍhā 'bhrāntapratyakṣadarśanāt nirākāra: paramāṇudharmatātītaḥ kalpanārahitatvāt pihitāpihitanetragamyaḥ
evaṃ sarvākāranirākāro hetuḥ prajñāpāramitā śūnyatā sarvākāra 'varopetā tadutpannaṃ phalaṃ paramākṣarasukhaṃ ṣoḍaśārddhā- ībindudhuk mahāprajñājñānamityucyate tathāgataiḥ
uktañca- sarvākāravaropetā śūnyatā hetuno ditā | anālambā kṛpā paścājjagadarthakarī phalam || iti
ṣoḍaśānāṃ kalānāmarddhamaṣṭo tadarddhaṃ catvāro bindavaḥ kāyavāścittajñānalakṣaṇāḥ, jāgratsvapnasuṣuptituryāvasthājanakāḥ
tān dhārayatīti ṣoḍaśārddhārddhabindudhṛk
samaya- catuṣṭayapālakaḥ vajrasattvo mahārāga ityarthaḥ
akala : śuklapakṣapañcadaśakalārahitaḥ tāsāmante sthitaḥ śuklapūrṇimāvasāne kalanātīta iti
kalanā kṛṣṇapratipat tatrā- pratiṣṭhitaḥ kalanātītaḥ sahaja ityarthaḥ
caturthadhyānakoṭighṛgiti prathamamānandasukhadhyānaṃ, dvitīyaṃ paramānandasukhadhyānaṃ, tṛtīyaṃ viramānandasukhadhyānaṃ, caturthaṃ sahajānandasukhadhyānam
pañcamī daśamī pūrṇā
pūrṇānte bindo paramākṣarasukhadhyānaṃ sthitaṃ tasya koṭiragrabhāgaḥ sukhaparipūrṇatā tāṃ gharatīti caturthadhyānakoṭighṛk || 3 ||
sarvaṃdhyānakalābhijñaḥ samādhikula gotravit
samādhikāyaḥ kāyāgryaḥ sarvasaṃbhoga kāyarāṭ ||4||
sarvadhyāneti
jñeyaṃ jñānodayastaka vicārastatprayogataḥ
tṛtīyaṃ prītisaṃkāśaṃ caturthaṃ sukhasaṃgrahaḥ
pañcamaṃ tu sadālokaṃ nirabhraṃ gaganasannibham || iti
tu pañcadhyānakalābhijñatvāt sarva kalābhijñaḥ
sarvadharmasamatādisamādhisamūha- sāmarthyavetṛtvāt samādhikulagotravit
samādhikāyo dharmakāyo'yameva sambhoganirmāṇa- kāyayoragyo yasya sa tathā
dharmakāyābhinnasaṃbhogakāyena sarvaparṣanmadhye rājata iti sarvasambhogakāyarāṭ || 4||
bhrāntā
varopitā
phala ruditaḥ, gotraghṛk
kāya koṭivibhāvakaḥ
guhyaṃ tarkoṃdayaṃ tarka vicāraṃ
mṛtakaṇikāsahitā nirmāṇakāyaḥ kāyāgyo buddhanirmāṇa vaṃśadhṛk
daśadigviśvanirmāṇo yathāvajjagadarthaṃkṛt ||5||
nirmāṇakāyeti
anābhogabahirjagadarthanānāprakṛtiko nirmāṇakāya evānyeṣāṃ nirmāṇānāṃ madhye'grayo nirmāṇakāyakāyāyyaḥ
sarvabhāvasahita tri kulapañcakulādivaṃ- śasya prabhāsvaratvena dhāraṇāttathā
daśadikṣu viśvasya nirmāṇaspharaṇaṃ yasya sa tathā
yathā svajagadarthakaraṇād yathāvajjagadarthakṛt || 5 ||
devātidevo 'devendraḥ surendro dānavādhipaḥ
amarendraḥ suraguruḥ pramathaḥ pramatheśvaraḥ ||6||
devātideveti
mahārāgātmakakāyatvena brahmātmakatvād devātidevaḥ
prabhāsvara - cittavastratvena viṣṇusvabhāvatvād devendraḥ
ratnāntaruṣṇīṣagamanena balirūpatvāt surendraḥ
anupamādvayaśrīdharmāmṛtalābhena rāhusvabhāvatvād dānavādhipaḥ
nairātmye airāvatagatitvena purandarasvabhāvatvādamarendraḥ
niḥsvabhāvī sarvadharma prakāśanena bṛhaspatisvabhāvatvāt suraguruḥ
mahāsukhaśukradānta vaśīkṛtaprādeśikakṣaṇā divighnagaṇatvena vighnādhi - patvātpramathaḥ
kāyavākcittamuktirūpatryambakasvabhāvatvāt pramayeśvaraḥ || 6 ||
uttīrṇabhavakāntāra ekaḥ śāstā jagadguruḥ

uttorṇabhaveti

eko'dvitīyaḥ

āryamañjuśrī nāmasaṅgītiḥ mārārirmārajidvīraścaturmārabhayāntakṛt sarvamāracamūjetā sambuddho lokanāyakaḥ ||9||
mārāroti
prabhāsvararatnākrāntakāyavākcittarāgatvena mārāriḥ
vajrasattvena prāṇāpānanirodhānmārajivajayaḥ ata eva boraḥ
maitryādisvabhāvatvājjāgratsvapna- suṣuptituryākṣayāccaturmārabhayāntakṛt
prakṛtinirmalasvādhiṣṭhānatayā sarvamāracamūjetā
ṣaṇmaṇḍalādhipatitvāt sambuddhaḥ
bhavarāgatṛṣṇāmalaprahīṇatvāllokanāyakaḥ || 9 ||
vandyaḥ pūjyo'bhivādyaśca mānanīyaśca nityaśaḥ
' arcanīyatamo mānyo namasyaḥ paramo guruḥ || 10 ||
vandya iti
śubhatīrthajñānapraveśenāvidyādimalaprakṣālanena sadevakānāṃ lokānāṃ paramo guruḥ || 10 ||
trailokye kakramagatirvyoma paryantavikramaḥ traividyaḥ śrotriyaḥ pūtaḥ ṣaḍabhijña: ṣaḍanusmṛtiḥ || 11||
trailokyeketi
trailokyakakramasyādvitīyasahajānandasya gatiranubhavastena vā gati- vrvyāptirasya
vyomni paryanto vikramaḥ sahajarūpatayā krīḍanaṃ yasya sa tathā
nirābhāsasya spharaṇarūpatayā rūpa iti yāvat
tisro vidyāḥ kāmāvacararūpāvacaramahāmudrāsiddhi sādhikā yasya sa eva traividyaḥ
śrutimātreṇāpi paramasukhakāritvena suviśuddhatvāt śrotriyaḥ
caturtharūpeṇa nityodayāt pūtaḥ pavitraḥ
tadgrāhyagrāhakaikatvenādvayajñānitvāt ṣaḍabhijñaḥ
ṣaṭcakrasamādhipraviṣṭadānādipāramitāghāraṇasmaraṇarūpatvāt ṣaḍanusmṛtiḥ || 11 ||
bodhisattvo mahāsattvo lokātīto mahaddhikaḥ
prajñāpāramitāniṣṭhaḥ prajñātattvatvamāgataḥ || 12||
bodhisattveti
sarva dharmānāsaktamahāyānārthāvabodhād bodhisattvaḥ
'aparimita - sukhena sattvatarpaṇānmahāsattvaḥ
laukikasaṃkalparahitatvāllokātītaḥ
acintyapañca- • • :: amṛtakaṇikāsahitā kāmopabhogasukhena sattvoddharaṇamahāprabhāvāt ( vatvāt ) mahaddhikaḥ
'sucandratvena sarvopa- ..lambhavigamāt prajñāpāramitāniṣṭhaḥ
prajñātastvaṃ mahāmudrā' svarūpamāgataḥ prāptaḥ || 12 ||
ātmavitparavitsarvaḥ sarvīyo hyagrapudgalaḥ
sarvopamāmatikrānto jñeyo jñānādhipaḥ paraḥ ||13||
ātmaviditi
sarvaprapañcātikrāntatvena gaganarūpatvādātmavit
svaparayora- dvaitabodhāt paravit
aśeṣabhāvasvabhāvasaṃdarśanāt sarvaḥ
bhāvābhāvayoradvaitabodhatvāt sarvīyaḥ
mahāsukhātmakajñānakāyatvādaprapudgalaḥ
anupamadharmakāyarūpatvāt sarva- lokopamāmatikrāntastu sarvopamāmatikrāntaḥ
sahajatvena pratyātmavedyatvājjñeyaḥ
sukhagrāhakatvena jñānādhipatyājjñānādhipaḥ
ata eva paraḥ || 13 ||
dharmaṃdānapatiḥ śreṣṭhaścaturmudrārthadeśakaḥ
paryupāsyatamo jagatāṃ niryāṇatrayayāyinām ||14||
dhamaṃdāneti yathābhavyatā ( tayā ) aviparītadharmadānād dharmadānapatiḥ
manaso'pi tyāgād dharmadānapatitvena śreṣṭhaḥ
karmamudrā - mahāmudrā - jñānamudrā- samayamudrārthasya deśaka:
prakāśakaḥ
caturmudrārthaprakāśanādeva niryāṇatraya yāyināṃ bhūcarakhecara mahāmudrā siddhi- gāmināṃ jagatāṃ paryupāsyaḥ
uktañca caturmudropadeśe, tadyathā- karmajñānamahāmudrāsamayākhyaḥ prabhāsvaraḥ
heturbhāvyā tathā prāpyā caturtho'vinaśvaraḥ
heturiti prathamaṃ karmamudrodbhūtaṃ yatsahajamacyutasukham
uktañca bhagavatā tatsatyameva, kintu saṃvṛtyā laukikadṛṣṭāntenā' darśamiva na paramārthataḥ
tasmāttīkṣṇendriyeṇa 'na grāhyā karmamudrā
karmamudrā ca stanakeśavato kāmadhātusukhasya hetuḥ
karma cumbanāliṅga- naguhyasparśa vastra sphālanādivyāpārātmakaṃ tenopalakṣitā mudrā pratyayakāriṇī
pratyayo'tra kṣarasukhalakṣaṇaḥ
mudaṃ sukhaviśeṣaṃ rāti dadāti iti mudrā bhāvyā
jñānamudrā, sā ca sva- cittaparikalpitā viśvamātrādidevīsvabhāvā pūrvānubhūtā rūpadhātusukhasya hetuḥ
jñānaṃ pūrva- hasita ramitādibhāvanā lakṣaṇaṃ, pratyayo'tra spandasukhalakṣaṇaḥ
skandhadhātvāyatanādidevatā- kāreṇa viśodhya maṇḍalacakrasvabhāvaṃ yathā vidhinā sphuṭīkṛtya manthamanthānayogena jñāna- vahni prajvālya yāvad dagdhe'haṃ sravate śaśī lalāṭakaṇṭhahunnābhivajrakamalakaṇi kāgrataḥ
āryamañjuśrīnāmasaṅgītiḥ bodhicittarūpeṇa viṣayendriyasamastamahāsukhasāgarasya yadekalolī 'bhūtatanmayasamāghimā ' panno bhūtvā tiṣṭhet yāvat śrīmahāmudrā'bhimukhī bhavatīti prāpyā
mahāmudrā svapnendrajālamano- mayasadṛśī
mahatī cāso [ mudrā ] mahāmudrā ceti kṛtvā mahattvaṃ punarasyāḥ sarvākāravaro- petatvaṃ na prādeśikatvam
mudrayate'neneti bodhicittavatreṇa mudrā
pratyayo'tra svacitta - parikalpanādharmarahito dhūmādinimittapūrvakaḥ svacittapratibhāso yogigamyaḥ pratisenā- vaditi
tāmāliṅgaya yāvatsamayamudrā sākṣāt kriyate
avinaśvarā paramākṣarasva- rūpiṇī sāca phalarūpamahāmudrā
mudaṃ paramākṣarasukhalakṣaṇaṃ rāti sarvakālamā 'datte pūrṇāvasthāyāmacalayogeneti mudrā
mahattvaṃ cāsyāḥ prahāṇamahattvenāghigamamahattvena
yatra prahāṇamahattvaṃ 'savāsanasarvāvaraṇaprahāṇalakṣaṇa svabhāvakāyākhyaprabhāsvarasākṣāt karaṇalakṣaṇam
adhigamamahattvaṃ tu pariśuddhasarvabuddhadharmātmakayuganaddhākhya'kāyasākṣāt karaṇam
yatpunaḥ śaradamalamadhyāhnagaganaṃ sannibhaṃ śūnyate karasa prabhāsvarasākṣātkāra eva sakalabuddhadharmādhigamo'śeṣaviśvārthasampādanarūpeṇa
tatra sarvabuddhadharmāṇāṃ bimba (dya)- mānatvāt
ata eva dharmāṇāmāśrayaḥ kāyo dharmakāya ityācakṣate
tatra ca pratibhā- senāpyadhigamavyavasthāyāṃ ghaṭapratibhāse ghaṭādyadhigamo prasaṅgo durnivāra:
kiñca sāṃkhya- darśanamadūṣaṇaṃ syāt
api ca, māyāsvapnagandharvapurapratisenā vat kalpitamaśeṣaskandhadhā- svāyatanādidarśanaṃ saṃvṛtisatyadarśanaṃ svādhiṣṭhānaṃ cocyate
saṃvṛtisatyameva paramārthasatyaṃ prabhāsvarapariśuddhamādarśādirūpaṃ vairocanādyādheyadevatāvṛndaṃ kūṭāgārādyādhāramaṇḍalaṃ ca tadeva ca lokottaramaṇḍalatayā sarvatantrarājeṣu gīyate
samastabuddhadharmasvabhāvatayā ca tadeva satyadvayādvaidhobhāvasvabhāvaṃ yuganaddhākhyamucyate
tasmādyuganaddhakāya eva dharmakāya:
sāṃbhogika svābhāvikakāyābhyāṃ pṛthagbhūto yogipratyakṣavedyaḥ
uktañca nāgārjunapādeḥ- “yo naiko nāpyaneka ityādinā dharmakāyalakṣaṇam
lokātītāmacintyām ityā- dinā sambhogakāyasya
satvānāṃ pākahetoḥ kvacidanala iva bhāti yo dīpyamānaḥ ityādinā nirmāṇakāyasya
trailokyācāramuktam ityādinā mahāsukhakāyasya
uktañca svadarśanamatoddeśe - rūparāśirananto me nirmāṇakāya uttamaḥ
rutarāśirananto me sambhogakāya uttamaḥ
dharmarāśirananto me dharmakāyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ
sukharāśirananto me sukhakāyo'kṣaraḥ paraḥ
iti
amṛta kaṇikāsahitā evañca ṣoḍaśīkalābodhaḥ paracittajñānapratiśabdasadṛśaśabdādhigamāśeṣarūpasanda- rśanajñānalakṣaṇaṃ catuḥ kāyasvarūpamāveditam
uktañca śrīkālacakre - na prajñā nāpyupāyaḥ sahajatanuriyaṃ dharmakāyo babhūva prajñopāya' svabhāvaḥ khaluvigatatamo jñānavijñānabhedāt
so'yaṃ saṃbhogakāya:
pratiravaka ivānekasattvārthakarttā sattvānāṃ pākahetorbhavati punarasī buddhanirmāṇakāyaḥ
iti kāvāścittajñānaviśuddhayā mudrācatuṣṭayamuktam
śrīādibuddhe coktam- abje vajrapraveśaḥ śikhini ca maruto bindupātastṛtīyaḥ etadyogatrayasya prakaṭataniyatā kāyavākcittamudrā
rāgārāgāntakā dyā paramasukhanidhiyoṃgagamyā caturthī mudrāṇāṃ seva mātā bhavati suphaladā śrīgurorvaktrameṣā
iti
uktañca vairocanābhisambodhitantre- anyatra ca - karmamudrāṃ samāsādya dharmamudrāṃ vibhāvayet
'tatra ūrdhvaṃ mahāmudrā tasyāḥ samayasambhavaḥ
iti
tāṃ mokṣalakṣmīmavinaṣṭasaukhyīṃ tyaktvā śubhāṃ bhagavatīṃ yuvati ramante
prāyeṇa pūrvā'jjitamugrakamaṃ yenāmṛtaṃ tyajya viṣaṃ pibanti
iti
śrīāvibuddhe ca- etā mudrāścatasro'kṣarasukhaphaladā yoginā bhāvanīyāḥ sarvasmin sarvakālaṃ surataratigatairlokamārga prayuktaiḥ

• āryamañjuśrī nāmasaṅgītiḥ paramārthaviśuddhaśrīstrelokyasubhago mahān

iti kṛtyānuṣṭhāna' jñānagāthāḥ pañcadaśa, paramārtheti
paramārthe jñānena viśuddhavīradvaya dhīryasya sa tathā
sarvasattvārtha- karaṇāt trailokyasubhagaḥ
vyāpitvaṃ mahān
sarvasukhasampadādhārabhūtatvāt sarvaṃ sampa- tkaraḥ
sarva yogināṃ priyaratnācyutatvācchrīmān
sarvadharmādvayātmakatvānmañjuśrī- radvayaśrīḥ
jñānakāyatvena śrīmatāṃ vara iti
nirāvaraṇapañcadaśakalāviśuddhayā sarvatathāgata- samādhigatakāryakāraṇalakṣaṇakṛtyānuṣṭhāna- jñānagāthāvyākhyā
pañcatathāgatastutiḥ namaste varada vajrāgrya bhūtakoṭe namo'stu te
namaste śūnyatāgarbha buddhabodhe namo'stu te
buddharāga namaste'stu buddhakāma namo namaḥ
buddhaprīte namastubhyaṃ buddhamoda namo namaḥ
buddhasmita namastubhyaṃ buddhahāsa namo namaḥ
buddhavāca namastubhyaṃ buddhabhāva namo namaḥ
abhavodbhava namastubhyaṃ namaste buddha saṃbhava
gaganodbhava namastubhyaṃ namaste jñāna sambhava
māyājāla namastubhyaṃ namaste buddhanāṭaka
namaste sarvasarvebhyo jñānakāya namo'stu te
iti pañcatathāgata stutijñānagāthāḥ pañca
evaṃ ca vajrasattvādidvāreṇa sṛṣṭikramanirdeśaḥ
ityanantarāntarā vyatikramanirde- śastu āmnāyagopanārthaḥ
anāhatanādollāsatvena sattvāśayavaśena tathā pratibhāsādvā saṃgītikārastathaiva likhyata iti
idānīṃ 'namaste' ityādipañcagāthābhistameva bhagavantaṃ vajradhātumahāmaṇḍalānta- rvottanaṃ pañcajñānamukhena stuvannāha - tatrādarśajñānadvāreṇa namaste varadetyādi, buddharāge- tyādi samatājñānamukhena, buddhasmitetyādi pratyavekṣaṇājñānamukhena, abhavodbhavetyādi kṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānātmanā, māyājāletyādi suviśuddhadharmadhātujñānasvabhāvena
vairocana- ratnasaṃbhavāmitābhāmoghasiddhayakṣobhyarūpatathāgatasvabhāvena ityarthaḥ
tatra prathamaślokena kāraṇamuktaṃ śeṣaiśca kāryamiti
tathā ca hetujñānaṃ pratyayajñānaṃ 'dharmajñānaṃ bhāvanājñānaṃ muktijñānaṃ ca
heturādarśajñānam, pratyayaḥ samatājñānam, pratyavekṣaṇā dharmajñānam, kṛtyānuṣṭhānaṃ
āyammañjuśrīnāmasaṅgītiḥ bhāvanājñānam, muktijñānaṃ suviśuddhadharmadhātujñānam
evaṃ ca pañcākārābhisambodhi- bhāvanā 'sūcitā
tena mūlatantre pañcākārajñānastaveṃ pañcaślokaiḥ pañcākāra bhāvanā bhagavatoktā, tadyathā— śūnyo bhāvasamūho'yaṃ kalpanārūpavarjitaḥ
dṛśyate pratiseneva kumāryā darpaṇe yathā || iti
lokottarasatyaṃ rūpaskandhādarśajñānam
sarvabhāvasamo bhūtvā eko bhāvo'kṣaraḥ sthitaḥ
akṣarajñānasaṃbhūto nocchedo na ca śāśvataḥ || iti
vedanāskandhaḥ samatājñānam
sarvasaṃjñātmakā varṇā akārakulasambhavāḥ
mahākṣarapadaprāptā na saṃjñā na ca saṃjñinaḥ || iti
saṃjñāskandhaḥ pratyavekṣaṇājñānam
anutpanneṣu dharmeṣu saṃskārarahiteṣu ca
na bodhirnaiva buddhatvaṃ na sattvo naiva jīvitam || iti
saṃskāraskandhaḥ kṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānam
vijñānadharmatātītā jñānaśuddhā hyanāvilāḥ
prakṛtiprabhāsvarā dharmāḥ dharmadhātugatiṃ gatāḥ || iti vijñānaskandhaḥ suviśuddhadharmadhātujñānam
• tathā laghutantre'pyuktam ekottaraśatādivṛttatrayeṇa cakracihnāditathāgataskandha- lakṣaṇam
tadyathā- cakraṃ svacchaṃ samantāttribhava iti sukhaṃ ratnamasyaiva rāgaḥ padmaṃ kleśakṣayo'siḥ kuliśamapi mahājñānakāyo hyabhedyaḥ
chedo'jñānasya kartrī varaṣaḍapi kulānyebhirutpāditā ye te'pyevaṃ veditavyāḥ khamiva samarasāḥ skandhadhārivandriyādyāḥ ||
yasminve jātirūpaṃ vrajati nidhanatāṃ tanmahārūpamuktam yasyāṃ saṃsāraduḥkhaṃ vrajati nidhanatāṃ sā mahāvedanoktā
yasyāṃ saṃsārasaṃjñā vrajati nidhanatāṃ sā mahāvajrasaṃjñā yasmin saṃsāravṛddhirvrajati nidhanatāṃ vaṣtrasaṃskāra eva ||
yasmin nidrādyavasthā vrajati nidhanatāṃ tacca vijñānamuktaṃ yasminnajñānabhāvo vrajati nidhanatāṃ tanmunerjñānameva
ete vairocanādyāḥ paramajinavarā: ṣaḍvidhāḥ ṣaṭkulāni anye ṣaḍdhātubhedā avaniśikhipayo mārutākāśaśāntāḥ || iti
arthastu - caturānandasvabhāvatvādvaradavajrāgrya iti sambodhanam
tvena bhūtakoṭīti ārṣatvāt
sukhaśūnyādvayatvena śūnyatāgarbhaḥ
dṛṣṭāntarūpatvena buddhabodhiḥ prabhāsvarapraveśo namaskāraḥ
mahārāgarūpatvād buddharāgaḥ
viramānandatvena dharmakāyatvād buddhakāyaḥ
buddhairvā kāmyata iti buddhakāma [ iti ] pāṭhaḥ
śūnyatāgarbhasahajānanda- kṣaṇatvena buddhaprītiḥ
dvayaprahāṇenādvayasukhapramodalābhād buddhamodaḥ
etena pratyayajñāna- muktam
'sukhamayajñānaprakāśakayogād buddhasmitaḥ
sahajacaṇḍālījyotiṣā sakalajñeya- maṇḍalavyāpanād buddhahāsaḥ
karmāṅganādvārāpi avadhūtīpada prāpitaprāṇādivāyutvena anāhatanādollāsarūpatvād buddhavāca:
'dharmakāyābhinnasambhogatvena buddhabhāvaḥ
śūnyatā- sambhūtacatuḥ kāyatvāghabhavodbhavaḥ
svābhāvikatvād buddhasaṃbhavaḥ
svayambhūrūpatvād gaganoda- turyātītāstināstivyatikrāntasvasaṃvedyatvājjñānasaṃbhavaḥ
mahāmāyāsamud- bhūtaparamasukhatvena māyājāla:
sahajasphuratsthiracalarūpatvena buddhanāṭakaḥ
sarvatra sambodhanam
ṣoḍaśānandarūpatvena bahutvātsarvaṃ sarvebhyaḥ
samantabhadra mahāsukhajñānakāya- syāgratvājjñānakāya iti || bhavaḥ
- iti pañcatathāgatajñānastutigāthā: pañca || j anuśaṃsā iyamaso vajrapāṇe: vajragharabhagavato jñānamūrteḥ sarvatathāgatajñāna- kāyasya mañjuśrījñānasattvasyāveṇikapariśuddhā nāmasaṅgītistavānuttaraprīti- prāsādamahodvilyasaṃjananāthaṃ, kāyavāṅmano' guhyapariśuddhaye, aparipūrṇapari- śuddhabhūmipāramitāpuṇyajñānasaṃbhāraparipūri śuddhaye, anadhigatānuttarārthasyā- ghigamāya, aprāptasya prāptye, yāvatsavaṃtathāgata sarvadharmanetrīsaṃdhāraṇārthaṃ ca mayā deśitā, saṃprakāśitā, vivṛtā, vibhajitottānīkṛtā, adhiṣṭhitā ceyaṃ mayā vajrapāṇe vajjradhara tava santāne citte sarvamantradharmatā ghiṣṭhāneneti
iti prathama cakrasyeyamanuśaṃsā ekādaśa padāni || punara paramiyaṃ vajrapāṇe vajradhara nāmasaṅgītiḥ supariśuddhā, paryavadātā, sarvajñajñānakāyavāṅmanoguhyabhūtā, sarvatathāgatānāṃ buddhabodhiḥ, dharmadhātugatiḥ samyaksaṃbuddhānāmabhisamayaḥ, sarvatathāgatānāmanuttaraḥ, sugatānām, sarvamārabala parājayo jinānām, daśabalabalitā sarvadaśa- balānām, sarvajñatā sarvajñajñānānām, āgamaḥ sarvadharmāṇām, samudāgamaḥ sarva buddhadharmāṇām, vimalasupariśuddhapuṇyajñānasaṃbhārapari pūrī sarva mahābodhi- sattvānām, prasūtiḥ sarvaśrāvakapratyekabuddhānām, kṣetraṃ sarvadevamanuṣyasampatteḥ, pratiṣṭhā mahāyānasya, saṃbhavo bodhisattvacaryāyāḥ, niṣṭhā samyagāryaṃmāgaṃsya ' nikaṣo vimuktīnām, utpatti' nirvāṇa māgaṃsya, anucchedastathāgatavaṃśasya, pravṛddhirmahābodhi 'sattvakulagotrasya, nigrahaḥ sarvaparapravādinām, vidhvaṃsanaṃ sarvatīthikānām, parājayaścaturmārabalacamū senāyāḥ saṃgrahaḥ sarvasattvā- nām, āryamāgaṃ paripākaḥ sarvaniryāṇayāyinām, samādhiścaturbrahmavihāra- vihāriṇām, dhyānamekāgracittānām, yogaḥ kāyavāṅmano'bhiyuktānām visaṃyogaḥ sarvasaṃyojanānām, prahāṇaṃ sarvakleśopakleśānām, 'vyupaśamaḥ sarvāvaraṇānām, vimuktiḥ sarvabandhanānām, mokṣaḥ 'sarvopadhīnām, śāntiḥ sarvacittopaplavānām, ākaraḥ sarvasampattīnām, parihāṇiḥ sarva- vipattīnām, vidhānaṃ sarvāpāyadvārāṇām, satpayo vimukti purasya, apravṛttiḥ saṃsāracakrasya pravartanaṃ dharmacakrasya, ucchritacchatradhvaja patākā tathāgataśāsanasya, adhiṣṭhānaṃ sarvadharma deśanāyāḥ, kṣiprasiddhimantramukhacaryā - cāriṇāṃ bodhisattvānām, bhāvanādhigamaḥ prajñāpāramitābhiyuktānāṃ, bodhisattvānām, śūnyatāprati vedho'dvayaprati 'vedabhāvanābhiyuktānām, niṣpattiḥ sarvapāramitāsaṃbhārasya, pariśuddhiḥ sarvabhūmipāramitā pari- pūryāṇām, prativedhaḥ samyakcaturāryasatyānām, sarvadharmaika cittaprativedhaścatu:- yāvacchandaparisamāptiḥ smṛtyupasthānānām, sarvabuddhaguṇānāmiyaṃ nāmasaṅgītiḥ
iti dvitīyacakrasyeyamanuśaṃsā padāni dvāpañcāśat || punaraparaṃ vajrapāṇe vajradhara iyaṃ nāmasaṅgītiḥ sarvasattvānāma- sarvasattvānāṃ sarvāpāya- śeṣakāyavāṅmanaḥ samudācārapāpapraśamanī, viśodhanī, sarvadurgatinivāraṇī, sarvakarmāvaraṇasamucchedanakarī, sarvā- ṣṭakṣaṇasamutpādasyānutpādanakarī, aṣṭamahābhayavyupaśamanakarī, sarvaduḥ- svapna vināśanakarī, sarva durnimittavyapohanakarī, du:śakunavighnavyu- paśamanakarī, sarvamārārikamaṃdūrīkaraṇī, sarvakuśalamūlapuṇyopacayakarī, sarvāyoniso (śo) manasikārasyānutpādanakarī, sarvamadamānadarpāha āryamañjuśrī nāmasaṅgītiḥ
ṅkāranirghātanakarī, sarvaduḥkhadaurmanasyānutpādanakarī, sarvatathāgatānāṃ hṛdaya- bhūtā, sarvabuddhabodhisattvānāṃ guhyabhūtā, sarvaṃśrāvakapratyekabuddhānāṃ rahasya- bhūtā, sarvamudrāmantrabhūtā, sarvadharmāna bhilāpyavādināṃ smṛtisaṃprajanyasaṃjananī, anuttaraprajñāmedhākarī, ārogyabaleśvaryaṃ sampatkarī, śrīśubhaśāntikalyāṇa- pravardhanakarī, yaśaḥ kīrtiślokastuti prakāśanakarī, sarvavyādhimahābhaya- vyupaśamanakarī, pūtatarā pūtatarāṇām, pavitratarā pavitratarāṇām, ghanyatamā dhanyatamānām, sarvamāṅgalyatamā sarvamāṅgalyatamānām, śaraṇaṃ śaraṇārthinām, layanaṃ layanārthinām, trāṇaṃ trāṇārthinām, parāyaṇaṃ parāya- ṇānām, dvīpabhūtā dvopārthinām, agatikānāmanuttaragatibhūtā, yānapātra- bhūtā bhavasāgarapāragāminām, mahābhaiṣajyarājabhūtā sarvavyādhinirghātana- tayā, prajñābhūtā heyopādeyabhāva vibhāvanatayā, jñānālokabhūtā sarvatamo- nvakāra kudṛṣṭyapanayanāya, cintā maṇirājabhūtā sarvasattvāśayābhiprāya- paripūraṇāya, sarvajñajñānabhūtā mañjuśrījñānakāya pratilambhāya, pariśuddhajñānā- darśabhūtā pañcacakṣupratilambhāya, ṣaṭpāramitāparipūrībhūtā āmiṣābhaya- dharmadānotsṛjanatayā daśabhūmipratilambabhūtā puṇyajñānasaṃbhārasamādhipari- pūraṇatayā, advayadharmatā dvayadharmaṃ vigatatvādatathatārūpatā, ananyadharmatādhyāro- pavigatatvāt, bhūtakoṭirūpatā pariśuddhasarvatathāgatajñānakāyasvabhāvatayā, sarvākāramahāśūnyatārūpatā aśeṣakudṛṣṭi gati gahana nirghātanatayā, sarvadharmāna- bhilāpya rūpeyaṃ nāmasaṃgītiḥ yadutādvaya dharmaṃtāvataraṇārthaṃ nāmasaṃghāraṇa- prakāśanatayā
| iti tṛtīyacakrasyeyamanuśaṃsā padāni dvāpañcāśat
| punaraparaṃ vajrapāṇe vajraghara kaścit kulaputro vā kuladuhitā vā mantramukhacaryācārī imāṃ bhagavato mañjuśrījñānasattvasya sarvatathāgata- jñānakāyasya bhagavato jñānamūrteradvayaparamārthaṃ nāmasaṅgīti nāmacūr̤āmaṇi kaṇikāsahitā sakalaparisamāptāmanyūnāmakhaṇḍāmebhireva gāthāpadavyañjanaiḥ pratyahaṃ trikālaṃ dhārayiṣyati, vācayiṣyati, paryavāpsyati, yoniśaśca manasi kariṣyati, parebhyaśca vistareṇa yathāsamayaṃ yathāvatsaṃprakāśayiṣyati, pratyekaṃ cānyatamānyatamaṃ nāmārthaṃ mañjuśrījñānakāyamālambanīkṛtya ekāgra- manasā bhāvayiṣyati, adhimuktitattvamanaskārābhyāṃ samanta mukhavihāra- vihārī sarvadharma prativedhikayā paramayā anāvilayā prajñāviddhayā śraddhayā samanvāgataḥ san tasya tryadhvābaddhasaṃginaḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvāḥ saṃgamya samāgamya sarvadharmaṃmukhānyupadarśayiṣyanti, ātmabhāvaṃ copadarśayiṣyanti, sarvabuddhabodhisattvādhiṣṭhānaṃ ca sarvakāyavāṅmanobhistasya santāne 'citte samyagaghiṣṭhāsyanti, sarvabuddhabodhisattvānugraheṇa cānugrahīṣyanti, sarvadharma - vaiśāradyapratibhānaścopasaṃhariṣyanti, sarvārhacchrāvakapratyekabuddhāścāryaṃ dharma- pramāṇatayātmabhāvaṃ copadarśayiṣyanti durdāntadamakāśca mahākrodharājāno mahāvajradharādayo jagatparitrāṇabhūtā nānānirmāṇakāryarojobalaṃ tejo'pra- dhṛṣyatāṃ sarvaṃ mantramudrābhisamayamaṇḍalānyu pasaṃhariṣyanti, aśeṣāśca mantramahā- vidyārājñyaḥ sarvavighnavināyakamārāripratyaṅgirā mahāparājitāḥ sarā- trindivaṃ pratikṣaṇaṃ sarveryāpatheṣu rakṣāvaraṇagupti kariṣyanti ye ca brahmendro - pendrarudranārāyaṇa sanatkumāra maheśvara kārtikeyamahākāla nandikeśvarayama varuṇa kuverahāratī daśa diglokapālāśca te sarve satata saṃmitaṃ rātrindivaṃ gacchatastiṣṭhataḥ śayānasya niṣaṇṇasya svapato jāgrataḥ samāhitasyāsamā- hitasya ca ekākinā bahujanamadhyagatasya yāvad grāmanagaranigamajanapada- rāṣṭra rājadhānīgatasya indrakīla rathyāpratolovīthīnagaradvāra rājamārga catvara śṛṅgāṭakanagarāntarāpaṇapaṇyaśālāmadhyagatasya yāvacchūnyāgāragirikandara- - āryamañjuśrīnāmasaṅgītiḥ nadī va gahanopagatasya ucchiṣṭasyānucchiṣṭasya mattasya pramattasya sadā sarvathā rakṣāvaraṇa gupti kariṣyanti
rātrindivaṃ ca paraṃ svastyayanaṃ kariṣyanti, ye cānye devanāgayakṣagandharvāsuragaruḍa kinnaramahoragamanuṣyāmanuṣyā ye cānye grahanakṣatra gaṇapatayo yāśca saptamātaro yāśca yakṣiṇīrākṣasīpiśācyastāḥ sarvāḥ sahitāḥ samagrāḥ sasenyāḥ saparivārāḥ sarveryāpatheṣu parāṃ rakṣāvaraṇa- gupti kariṣyanti paraṃ ca tasya kāye mojobalaṃ prakṣepsyanti ārogyamāyu- vṛddhi copasaṃhariṣyanti
| iti caturthacakrasyeyamanuśaṃsā padānyekonaviṃśatiḥ
| punaraparaṃ vajrapāṇe vajradhara ya imāṃ nāmasaṅgīti nāmacūḍāmaṇi pratyahamakhaṇḍasamāghānatastrikṛtvā kaṇṭhagatāmāvartayiṣyati, pustakagatāṃ vā paṭhamānaḥ pravartayiṣyati, bhagavato mañjuśrījñānasattvasya rūpamālambayan, tadrūpamanucintayan tadrūpamanuṣyāyan tameva rūpaṃ nirmāṇakāyenācirādeva vinayavaṃśamupādāya rakṣati, gaganatalagatāśca buddhabodhisattvāḥ nānā- nirmāṇarūpakāyeṃ: sahagatā rakṣanti, na ca tasya sattvasya jātu kathamapi durgatyapāyapatanaṃ bhaviṣyati, na nīcakulopapattirbhaviṣyati, na pratyanta- janapadopapattirbhaviṣyati na honendriyo bhaviṣyati, na mithyādṛṣṭikulo- papattirbhaviṣyati, nābuddhakeṣu buddhakṣetreṣūtpatsyate, na ca buddhotpādavi mukhatā taddeśitadharmavimukhaparokṣatā bhaviṣyati na ca dīrghāyuṣkeṣu deveṣūtpasyate, na ca durbhikṣarogaśastrāntaḥ kalpeṣūtpatsyate, na ca pañcakaṣāyakāleṣūtpatsyate, na ca rājaśatrucorabhayaṃ bhaviṣyati na sarvopakaraṇavaikalyadāridryabhayaṃ bhaviṣyati, nāślokābhyākhyānanindāyaśo'kīrtibhayaṃ bhaviṣyati, sujāti- kulagotra sampannaśca bhaviṣyati, samantaprāsādikarūpavarṇasamanvāgataśca bhaviṣyati, priyo mana āpaḥ paramasukhasaṃvāsaḥ, priyadarśanaśca lokānāṃ bhaviṣyati, śubhasaubhāgyādeyavākyaśca lokānāṃ bhaviṣyati, yatra yatro - amṛtakaṇikāsahitā papatsyate, tatra tatra jātismaro bhaviṣyati, mahābhoga: mahāparīvāro'kṣaya- parīvāraśca bhaviṣyati, agraṇīḥ sarvasattvānāmagraguṇasamanvāgato bhaviṣyati, prakṛtyā ca ṣaṭpāramitāguṇa samanvāgato bhaviṣyati, caturbrahmavihāra vihārī bhaviṣyati, smṛtisaṃprajanyopāyabalapraṇidhi jñānasamanvāgataśca bhaviṣyati, sarvaśāstraviśārado vāgmī ca bhaviṣyati, spaṣṭavāgajaḍaḥ paṭuśca bhaviṣyati, dakṣo'nalasaḥ saṃtuṣṭo mahārtho vitṛṣṇaśca bhaviṣyati, parama- viśvāsī ca sarvasattvānāmācārya gurusaṃmataśca bhaviṣyati, ca tasya sarvaśilpakalābhijñājñānaśāstrāṇi cārthato granthataśca pratibhā- samāgamiṣyanti, supariśuddha śolājīva samudācāracārī ca bhaviṣyati, supravrajitaḥ rūpasaṃpannaśca bhaviṣyati, apramuṣitasarvajñatā bodhicittaśca bhaviṣyati, na jātu śrāvakātpratyeka buddha niyamāva krāntigataśca bhaviṣyati
| iti pañcamacakrasyeyamanuśaṃsā pādānyekapañcāśat
| evaṃ vajrapāṇe vajradhara aprameyaguṇasamanvāgato'so mantramukhacaryā - cārī bhaviṣyati, anyeścāprameyerevaṃ prakārairguṇagaṇaiḥ samanvāgato bhaviṣyati, acirādeva vajrapāṇe vajradhara paramārthanāmasaṅgītisaṃdhārakaḥ puruṣapudgalaḥ susaṃbhṛtapuṇyajñānasaṃbhāraḥ kṣiprataraṃ buddhaguṇānsamudānīyānuttarāṃ samyaksaṃbo- cimabhisaṃbhotsyate analpakalpāparinirvāṇadharmāṇāṃ sarvasattvānāmanuttara- dharmaṃdeśako'dhiṣṭhātā daśadiksaddharma dundubhirdhamaṃgajaḥ

samyag bodhimārgatvāt [ sat]pathaḥ
pratītyasamutpādavicchedād apravṛtti:

pūtatarā puṇyajñānasambhārapūraṇena

anāthānāṃ śaraṇyatvāt śaraṇyam
ratihetutvāllayanam
svasvadharmeṇa śrāṇārthināṃ pālanāt trāṇam
paritrāṇavirahiṇāṃ parāyaṇam
nirvāṇapura- prāpaṇāt dvīpabhūtā
sarvākāranirākāraśūnyatāprakāśanena jñānālokabhūtā
pañca cakṣūṃṣi māṃsādīni
grāhyagrāhakābhāvādadvayadharmatā avadhūtīgatatvena sarvāropavigamāt tathatā - rūpatā
niḥsvabhāvatvāt sarvadharmānabhilāparūpā
mantramukhena śruti (ta) cintābhāvanācaryā caratīti tathā nāmnā cūḍāmaṇi: sarvatathāgatakulodbhavānāmiti nāmacūḍāmaṇiḥ
avikala buddhaguṇayogāt saphala pari- samāptā

yoniśaśca manasi kariṣyati bhāvanātaḥ

pañcaka- ṣāyāḥ sattvadṛṣṭikalpāyuḥ parisphāralakṣaṇāḥ
aślokābhyākhyāna nindāvādapūrvaka pratyā- khyānam
priyo mana āpo manogrāhī
adhiṣṭhito'na (nu ) vattino daśasu dikṣu ghamaṃdundubhiryasya sa tādṛśo dharmarāja iti dharmarājādhipatirbhaviṣyatīti sambandhaḥ
mantravinyāsaḥ oṃ sarvadharmābhāvasvabhāvaviśuddhavatra a ā aṃ aḥ prakṛtipari- śuddhāḥ sarva dharmāḥ yaduta sarvatathāgatajñānakāyasya mañjuśrīpariśuddhitā- mupādāyeti
'a āḥ sarvatathāgata hṛdaya hara hara oṃ hūṃ hrīṃ bhagavan jñāna- mūrtivāgīśvara mahāvāca sarvadharmagaganāmala supariśuddhadharmadhātujñānagabhaṃ āḥ iti mantravinyāsaḥ
oṃ sarvadharmābhāvasvabhāvaviśuddhavatra a ā aṃ aḥ prakṛtipariśuddhāḥ sarvadharmāḥ, yaduta sarvatathāgatajñānakāyasya mañjuśrī pariśuddhitāmupādāyeti
a āḥ sarvatathāgatahṛdaya hara hara oṃ hū~ hrīṃ bhagavan jñānamūrti ( teṃ) vāgīśvaramahāvāca sarvadharmagaganāmala supariśuddhadharmadhātujñānagarbha āḥ
ādimadhyāntādhiṣṭhāno'yaṃ mantraḥ
tathāhi - bodhicittamakāra:, bhūmipāramitā- caryāsvabhāvākāraḥ
buddhatvasūcakaḥ aṃkāraḥ
mahānirmā(ba) dyotako aḥkāraḥ
jñānakā- yādhiṣṭhānaka ākāraḥ
kāyavākcittarāgādhiṣṭhānakā oṃ hū~ hrīṃkārāḥ dharmadhātu- vāgīśvarātmakaṃ ( ka ) sthiracalarūpāvasthitaparidharmakāyāḥ vihita iti
mudrārthaḥ āḥkāro'nte atha vajradharaḥ śrīmān hṛṣṭatuṣṭaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ
praṇamya nāthaṃ saṃbuddhaṃ bhagavantaṃ tathāgatam

iti 'upasaṃhāragāthāḥ pañca
āryaṃ māyājālaṣoḍaśasāhasrikānmahāyogatantrāntaḥ pātisamādhi- jālapaṭalād bhagavatā śrīśākyamuninā bhāṣitā bhagavato mañjuśrījñāna- sattvasyādvayaparamārthānāmasaṅgītiḥ parisamāptā
ye dharmā hetuprabhavā hetuṃ teṣāṃ tathāgato hyavadat
teṣāṃ ca yo nirodha evaṃvādī mahāśramaṇaḥ
athetyanantaraṃ yaḥ prathamamadhyeṣitavīravajradharaḥ saṃprovācocceridaṃ vaca iti sambandhaḥ
tadātmasukhaniṣpatyā hṛṣṭaḥ, āyatisukhaniṣpatyā tuṣṭaḥ
samyaksaṃbodhiprāpakaḥ, śreyomārgaḥ, māyājālamantranītyoditaḥ
mahāniti svaparodayanibandhanārthaḥ

'abde vyomaviyat ṣaḍānanayute kṛṣṇe sacaitre ( site ) phālguṇe, ṣaṣṭhamyā anurādhake gurudine śuddhiśca yoge ime

punaḥ kiṃvidham ? ravītyādi

pañca- prakoṣṭhāni yasya tatpañcagatikaṃ saṃsāracakramasuraśaterdeveṣvantarbhāvāt

iti
dvikalparāje'pyuktam- na caryā bhogataḥ proktā yā khyātā bhīmarūpiṇī

evaṃ vidhamityādi
evaṃ śabdābhidheyaṃ pūrvoktameva vajravaramullā' layan vineyasantāne prabhāsvararaśmispharaṇena parārthopāyaṃ vyākhyāya svārtha vyācaṣṭe- athavetyādi
antyanimittena viśvabimbagatacittena

paramārtha- stastasya vikalpajalpayoragocaratvāt
ata evāha - yogināmeva gamya iti
pratyātmavedyaḥ imaṃ lokottarāyāḥ prabhāsvarekarasa mahāsukhābhisaṃbodherlabdhāyā dṛṣṭāntapratipādyamartha - mādhyātmikaṃ niścetumasamarthān pratyāha - naiśamityādi
vimaleti rakṣa ( kṣi ) tāstitvādi- samasta samāropakaluṣe bhūtakoṭi śūnyatāṃ kathayet
śiṣyaśca tena kṣaṇena bāhyasaṃbodhe- dṛṣṭāntabhūtāyā dṛṣṭyā niścayena prāpnoti, mahāsukham
paramārtharūpaṃ pratyayeti
taccādhyātma saukhyaṃ saṃvṛtirūpaṃ ca sarvaśūnyaṃ nimittānupūrvāyutam
nimittāni dhūmādīni
yogetyādyardhena dṛṣṭāntarūpakarmāṅganāyogasyācaraṇena yā bodhi: sā kathitā
parārdhena dāntikaścaturthaseka ucyate
catuḥ koṭimuktā tu bodhiścyuterante vināśo'sambhave vigatalakṣaṇā
satyupalambhe sukhārthino'cyutiḥ syādata eva niḥsvabhāvaścaturthaḥ
evamubhayeti
mahāsukhaniḥsvabhāvatādvayābhinnaṃ caturtham
ācāryādisekatrayaṃ hetuścaturthaṃ phalam
atra satāṃ vipratipattirayuktā
paramaviramamadhye lakṣyavādino vyañjana pratiśaraṇā evaṃ śabdasaṃgati phalatve kurvantu tanmate caturthatvaṃ punarasya ghaṭameva tṛtīyasya phalatvāt
kiṃ ca tṛtīyasya - caturthasya hetutvaṃ vaktavyam
taccāyuktaṃ hetubhiḥ phalārthaṃ mṛgyate phalaṃ cejjātaṃ kimanena
api ca seke turyamapi mahāmudrāsiddhyarthamuktam
phalasya siddhau sa sekaḥ kiṃprayojanaḥ
kiṃ ca niṣpanne'pi phale yadi seka ucyate tadā āśvāsayorbhedo na syāttadyathā sahajasukhaṃ tathāgata (tā) śvāsa stṛtīyaḥ tanniḥsvabhāvatvaṃ vajradharāśvāsa- ścaturthe
dvayorapi phalatvāt
api ca, tṛtīyaṃ phalamityasyānurodhādādau vajradharā- śvāsaḥ
paścādabuddhasyeti svīkṛtī prathamaṃ phalaṃ paścāddheturiti viparītaṃ prasajyeta
tadapyucyate - paramaviramayormadhye iti vacanādeva vyavasthāpyate mayeti
tanna, yato'sya vākyasyānya evārthaḥ
tataśca yathā tattvato gāthā'nyārthī
tathā 'lakṣyamapi bhavaduktādanyadeva kiṃ ca lakṣyānurodhātparamaviramayormadhye lakṣyaṃ vīkṣya dṛḍhī- kurvityasya lakṣaṇasya vyākhyā yuktā
na tu lakṣyānurodhāllakṣaṇamanyayā syāt
lakṣyaṃ dṛṣṭvā lakṣaṇa pravṛteḥ
api ca, samāja hevajjayostatsvo'bhinnārthatvāt sādhana- tṛtīyeḥ
I amṛtakaṇa kodyotanibandhaḥ mātrasyaiva bhedāccaturthameva phalam
tatra sekatrayamevoktamiti cenna laukikāstāvanta evetyāśayādevamuktam
caturthe'pyukto'sphuṭa: tṛtīyānusāreṇa tajjñātuṃ gururupāsya eva tatra
tasmātpūrvāparamapratisandhāya yathārutārthaṃ paratvena tṛtīyaṃ lakṣyaṃ vadantu nāmānye tathāpyāgamayuktibhyāmanta eva
phalasya caturthatve gāthārthaḥ kathamiti cet, na doṣo gāthārthaḥ saṅgata eva
ānandasahajayohi madhye paramaviramo bhavata ityatra na vivādaḥ
viśeṣastu madhye iti paramānandasahajābhyāṃ yojyam, na tvānandasahajayormadhye
tātparyā- thaṃsya yojanāyāṃ paramaviramayoriti ṣaṣṭhyantayorupādānaṃ vyarthaṃ na, ' ṣaṣṭhī cānādare' ityanādare ṣaṣṭhīyam
ayamarthaḥ - sahajānandayormadhyayoḥ paramaviramayoranādṛtya caturthaṃ lakṣyaṃ vīkṣyaṃ dṛḍhīkurvanti daśabhūmīśalokeśa khagarbhavajjrapāṇiyogīndrabhūtinā ropādaprabhṛti- bhirvyākhyātatvāt pratyeyam
viramasyānta iti kṛtvā suratānandādi gāthārddha pareṇa vyācaṣṭe etadityādinā
samastametadānandatrayaṃ suratānandasvabhāvaṃ suratodbhavatvāt
sahajasya hetubhūtam
asyeti caturthasya tadānandatrayaṃ sukhasvarūpamupāyo hetuḥ sākṣāt paramparayā ca
tattrayāṇāṃ vedakaḥ sahajānandaḥ phalañcaturtha eva
viramādyarddhagāthāṃ vyācaṣṭe viśiṣṭa ityādi
tṛtīyaṃ rāganāśatvādanupādeyamataścaturthameva bhāvyate
parametyādigāthāṃ vyācaṣṭe
paramānandaḥ kliṣṭanyagrūpatvādbhavaḥ
viramasya savikalpacyutirūpatvānnirvāṇa- svam
ānandastu madhyamo 'dhamo'pakṛṣṭo bhavasukhasamānatvāt
phalaṃ śāṃtādvaitama vikalpaṃ śaradamalamavyāhnagaganopamam
na rāgādyadhaṃ vyācaṣṭe rāga ityādi
āsaṅgarūpatvātsa- hajatvena nopalabhyata iti yojyam
viramo'pi na sukhavikalpena yogāt
madhyamāpi na

| 'āryanāmasaṅgītisādhanam namo mañjudhiye prathamaṃ tāvat sādhakaḥ prātarutthāya mukhaśaucādikaṃ kṛtvā dhyānagṛhaṃ praviśya sukhāsane paryaṅkenopaviśya svahṛtpadmacandre raktagauramuḥkāraṃ bhāvayet
tatastanmarīci - sañcayairaṅkuśākārairākṛṣya bhagavantaṃ vakṣyamāṇavarṇabhujacihnāsanopetaṃ purato vibhāvya manomayapuṣpadhūpādibhiḥ sampūjya tadagrataḥ pāpadeśanādikaṃ sarvaṃ vidhāya maṃtryādibrahmavihāra- catuṣṭayaṃ vibhāvya sarvadharmaśūnyatāmāmukhīkṛtya oṃ śūnyatājñānavastrasvabhāvātmako'hamiti mantreṇādhitiṣṭhet
tata ākāśe huṃkāreṇa pañcasūcikaṃ vastraṃ vibhāvya tadraśmibhiḥ yathāyogaṃ vaṣtraṃprākāraṃ vajrapañjaraṃ vajrāraṃ vajramayīṃ bhūmimārasātalamavalambya vastramaya- raśmībhūya vajraprākārād bahirgandhādirūpeṇāvasthitaṃ paśyet
tadanantaraṃ vajraprākārā- bhyantare hrīḥkāreṇa padmaṃ padmopari akāreṇa candramaṇḍalaṃ tadupari raktagauravarṇaṃ mu:kāra - manekabuddhasphuraṇasaṃharaṇākāraṃ vibhāvya tatpariṇataṃ mañjuśrībhaṭṭārakaṃ raktagauraṃ padmacandro- pari vajra paryaṅkaniṣaṇṇaṃ prathamamukhaṃ raktaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ nīlaṃ vāme śuklaṃ iti trimukhaṃ hastacatu- ṣṭayena yathāyogaṃ prajñākhaṅgadhanurbāṇayoginaṃ ratnakirīṭinaṃ dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītyanuvyañjana virājitaṃ kumāraṃ kumārābharaṇabhūṣitamātmānaṃ vibhāvya hṛdayapadmacandropari sattvaparyaṅka- niṣaṇṇāṃ sattvavatīṃ śuklāṃ vāme sparddhayā ghaṇṭādharāṃ dakṣiṇe hṛdyutkarṣaṇayogena vajradhāriṇīṃ ūrṇāyāṃ tathaivāvasthitāṃ ratnavatrīṃ pītāṃ karadvayena śirasi puṣpamālāṃ bandhayantīṃ kaṇṭhe padmavajra raktagorāṃ vāmena sagarvagṛhītapadmanālā dakṣiṇe tatpatravikāsinīṃ pūrvavadevāva - sthitāṃ mūrdhni karmavantrī haritavarṇāṃ trisūcikavastrānvitahastadvayenātmīyamūrddhānaṃ dhārayantīṃ tathāvasthitāṃ vibhāvya oṃ vajrāṅkuśajaḥ, oṃ vajrapāśa hu~, oṃ vastrasphoṭa vaṃ oṃ vaśīkṛtya vaṣtrāveśa ho: ityebhirmantraiḥ padmacandropari yathākramaṃ jñānamaṇḍalamākṛṣya praveśya vaddhā vajramuṣṭidvayaṃ baddhvā tarjanīkaniṣṭhābhiḥ śṛṅkhalāṃ kṛtvā racitavastracakramudrayā uttānataḥ svamukhavyavasthāpitayā arghyapādyapuṣpadhūpādibhiḥ sampūjya jhaṭiti svaśarīre praveśayet

mantraḥ - oṃ sarvadharmā- bhāvasvabhāvaviśuddhavastra a ā aṃ aḥ prakṛtipariśuddhāḥ sarvadharmā yaduta sarvatathāgata- jñānakāyamañjuśrīpariśuddhitāmupādāyeti a āḥ sarvatathāgatahṛdaya hara hara oṃ huṃ hrīṃ bhagavan jñānamūrtivāgīśvara mahāvāca sarvadharmagaganāmalasupariśuddhadharmadhātu jñānagarbha āḥ iti mantreṇādimadhyāvasānādhiṣṭhānapūrvakaṃ nāmasaṅgīti pratyahaṃ pratisandhyaṃ trīn vārān ekavāraṃ vā paryaṅkamabhinandan samāhitaḥ san paṭhet
